Not blocking ΓÇö this is a human task. ΓÜá NOTE: Subscription pricing in INR should be verified against current market rates before launch ΓÇö prices are estimates, not market-validated. They are included here to make the document set 100% complete for AI-driven development. 5.1 GDD Threshold Values by Crop and Stage These values must be stored in backend/data/gdd_thresholds.json and loaded by CropStageAgent. Crop Stage GDD from Planting Vulnerability to Disease Rice Germination 0-50 LOW Rice Seedling 50-200 LOW Rice Tillering 200-400 HIGH ΓÇö blast, brown spot, sheath blight Rice Panicle Initiation 400-600 HIGH ΓÇö blast, sheath rot Rice Flowering 600-750 CRITICAL ΓÇö blast, bacterial blight Rice Grain Fill 750-900 MEDIUM ΓÇö neck rot, sheath rot Rice Maturity 900-1100 LOW Maize Germination 0-60 LOW Maize Seedling 60-200 LOW Maize Vegetative (V6) 200-400 MEDIUM ΓÇö northern blight Maize Tasseling 400-600 HIGH ΓÇö northern blight, rust Maize Silking 600-750 HIGH ΓÇö smut, ear rot Maize Grain Fill 750-1000 MEDIUM Maize Maturity 1000-1200 LOW Tomato Seedling 0-150 LOW Tomato Vegetative 150-400 MEDIUM ΓÇö early blight Tomato Flowering 400-600 HIGH ΓÇö late blight, leaf curl Tomato Fruit Set 600-800 HIGH ΓÇö late blight, fruit rot Tomato Maturity 800-1000 MEDIUM Banana Establishment 0-300 LOW Banana Vegetative 300-800 MEDIUM ΓÇö sigatoka Banana Shooting 800-1200 HIGH ΓÇö fusarium, sigatoka Banana Flowering 1200-1600 HIGH ΓÇö fusarium, banana bunchy top Banana Maturity 1600-2000 MEDIUM Base temperatures: Rice=10┬░C, Maize=10┬░C, Tomato=10┬░C, Banana=15┬░C, Coconut=20┬░C 5.2 Disease Class Names for ONNX Vision Model Store in backend/data/disease_classes.json. These are the output labels from the vision model. Order must match the training class index exactly. Index Class Name (model output) Common Name Crop 0 rice_blast Rice Blast (Magnaporthe oryzae) Rice 1 rice_brown_spot Brown Spot (Bipolaris oryzae) Rice 2 rice_bacterial_blight Bacterial Blight (Xanthomonas oryzae) Rice 3 rice_sheath_blight Sheath Blight (Rhizoctonia solani) Rice 4 rice_leaf_folder Leaf Folder (Cnaphalocrocis medinalis) Rice 5 rice_healthy Healthy Rice Rice 6 banana_fusarium Fusarium Wilt (Fusarium oxysporum) Banana 7 banana_sigatoka Sigatoka Leaf Spot (Mycosphaerella) Banana 8 banana_bbtv Banana Bunchy Top Virus Banana 9 banana_healthy Healthy Banana Banana 10 coconut_wclwd Kerala Wilt (WCLWD) Coconut 11 coconut_bud_rot Bud Rot (Phytophthora palmivora) Coconut 12 coconut_healthy Healthy Coconut Coconut 13 tomato_early_blight Early Blight (Alternaria solani) Tomato 14 tomato_late_blight Late Blight (Phytophthora infestans) Tomato 15 tomato_leaf_curl Tomato Leaf Curl Virus Tomato 16 tomato_healthy Healthy Tomato Tomato 17 maize_northern_blight Northern Leaf Blight (Exserohilum turcicum) Maize 18 maize_common_rust Common Rust (Puccinia sorghi) Maize 19 maize_grey_leaf_spot Grey Leaf Spot (Cercospora zeae-maydis) Maize 20 maize_healthy Healthy Maize Maize 21 out_of_distribution Not a crop or unsupported crop OOD Save this file as backend/data/disease_classes.json. The ONNX model output index must match these exactly during training. 5.3 Crop-Stage-Disease Vulnerability Mapping Store in backend/data/stage_disease_map.json. Used by Critic Agent to detect stage-disease mismatches. Crop Stage Code Diseases Expected (HIGH risk) Diseases Unlikely (flag if detected) rice_germination None rice_blast, rice_brown_spot rice_seedling rice_leaf_folder rice_blast (possible but uncommon) rice_tillering rice_blast, rice_brown_spot, rice_leaf_folder rice_sheath_blight (rare at tillering) rice_panicle_initiation rice_blast, rice_sheath_blight None rice_flowering rice_blast, rice_bacterial_blight None rice_grain_fill rice_sheath_blight, rice_brown_spot rice_blast (uncommon post-flowering) maize_vegetative maize_northern_blight maize_common_rust (uncommon before tasseling) maize_tasseling maize_northern_blight, maize_common_rust None tomato_flowering tomato_late_blight, tomato_leaf_curl tomato_early_blight (less severe at flowering) tomato_fruit_set tomato_late_blight None 5.4 Malayalam Advisory Offline Templates ΓÇö Complete Store in backend/data/offline_templates_ml.json. Apply before writing any code. Create Firebase project and download google-services.json DAY 1 30 minutes Android push notifications require this file in the project. Create Cloudflare R2 bucket and get API credentials DAY 1 20 minutes Image upload fails without R2 credentials. Needed Phase 1 Task 1.5. Sign up at Oracle Cloud Always-Free and provision instance DAY 1 1 hour All backend runs here. Must be done before any deployment. Register at dssat.net and download DSSAT software BEFORE PHASE 3 1 hour Simulation agent needs DSSAT binary. Required for Task 3.1. Contact KAU Agricultural University for field image partnership WEEK 1 2 hours to draft email, ongoing Golden dataset requires expert-labelled field images. Cannot be automated. Download CIB&amp;RC pesticide label database and seed chemical_labels table BEFORE PHASE 1 LAUNCH 4 hours Chemical safety checker blocks all advisories until this table has data. Fill actual PMFBY and PM-KISAN eligibility rules into schemes table BEFORE PHASE 3 8 hours Scheme matching returns no results until scheme records exist in DB. 6.4 Build Start Sequence Follow exactly in this order: 1. Complete all 8 human tasks above ΓÇö or at minimum the first 4 (Day 1 tasks) 2. Open VSCode. Create new project folder. Initialize git repository. 3. Give VSCode AI agent: Implementation Plan (Doc 6) + Backend Schema (Doc 5) + Security (Doc 7) 4. Instruct AI: &apos;Execute Phase 0 Task 0.1 exactly as written in the Implementation Plan&apos; 5. Work through every task in Phase 0 sequentially. Do not skip. Verify exit criteria. 6. When a task involves LLM prompts: reference AI Prompt Handbook (Doc 8) for exact prompt text 7. When a task involves a screen: reference UI/UX Spec (Doc 4) + App Flow (Doc 3) together 8. When a task involves tests: reference QA Playbook (Doc 9) for test code 9. When a task involves security: reference Security (Doc 7) for implementation rules 10. Complete exit criteria for each phase before proceeding to next phase ΓÇö no exceptions 6.5 Document Reference Card ΓÇö Quick Lookup When AI needs... Prompts in this guide are copy-paste ready. Language Python (backend) ┬╖ Kotlin/Android (mobile) ΓÇö beginners can follow exactly Project Goal Launch Agri-SAGE as real production app used by Kerala farmers PART 1: FREE AI TOOLS ΓÇö SETUP FOR EVERY TEAM MEMBER 1.1 Which Free AI Tools to Use Tool Free Tier Best For How to Get VSCode + GitHub Copilot Free 2000 completions/month + chat free since Dec 2024 Writing code, completing functions, fixing errors Install VSCode from code.visualstudio.com ΓåÆ Install GitHub Copilot extension ΓåÆ Sign in with GitHub free account Cline (VSCode extension) Completely free with your own API key ΓÇö use Gemini API free tier Writing entire files, executing tasks step by step ΓÇö BEST for this project Install Cline extension in VSCode ΓåÆ Get free Gemini API key from aistudio.google.com ΓåÆ Connect Cline to Gemini Gemini (aistudio.google.com) Free ΓÇö Gemini 1.5 Pro with 1M token context window ΓÇö generous limits Reading all documents at once and generating large code blocks Go to aistudio.google.com ΓåÆ Free account ΓåÆ Create API key for Cline Claude.ai free tier Free ΓÇö limited messages per day Complex planning, reviewing code, understanding architecture claude.ai ΓåÆ free account GitHub Copilot Chat Free in VSCode Quick code questions, explaining errors Same as Copilot above ΓÇö already included Γ£ô BEST COMBINATION: Use Cline + Gemini 1.5 Pro free API for writing code files. Use GitHub Copilot free for code completion while typing. Use Claude free for planning and document review. This combination costs ZERO rupees. ΓÜá Free tiers have daily limits. If you hit a limit: stop for the day, continue tomorrow. Never pay for anything ΓÇö the free tiers are sufficient for building this project phase by phase. 1.2 Setup Every Team Member Must Do ΓÇö Day 1 Every team member does ALL of these steps on their own computer: STEP 1: Install VSCode Download from: code.visualstudio.com Install normally on Windows/Mac/Linux STEP 2: Install extensions in VSCode Open VSCode ΓåÆ Press Ctrl+Shift+X ΓåÆ Search and install: 1. GitHub Copilot (by GitHub) 2. Cline (by saoudrizwan) 3. Python (by Microsoft) 4. Pylance (by Microsoft) 5. ESLint (by Microsoft) 6. GitLens (by GitKraken) STEP 3: Get free Gemini API key for Cline Go to: aistudio.google.com Sign in with Google account (free) Click &apos;Get API key&apos; ΓåÆ Create API key ΓåÆ Copy it In VSCode: Click Cline icon (left sidebar) ΓåÆ Settings ΓåÆ API Provider: Google Gemini Paste your API key ΓåÆ Model: gemini-1.5-pro ΓåÆ Save STEP 4: Sign in to GitHub Copilot In VSCode: Click Copilot icon ΓåÆ Sign in with GitHub GitHub account is free ΓåÆ Copilot free tier activates automatically STEP 5: Install Git Download from: git-scm.com ΓåÆ Install In terminal: git config --global user.name &apos;Your Name&apos; git config --global user.email &apos;your@email.com&apos; STEP 6: Clone the project repository One team member creates GitHub repo: github.com ΓåÆ New repository ΓåÆ agrisage ΓåÆ Private All others: git clone https://github.com/TEAMLEAD/agrisage.git Copy ALL 11 documents into: agrisage/docs/ 1.3 How to Use Cline with Free Gemini ΓÇö The Main Build Tool Cline is the most powerful free tool. It reads your documents and writes entire files. Here is exactly how to use it: Step 1: Open VSCode in the agrisage project folder Step 2: Click the Cline icon in the left sidebar (looks like a robot) Step 3: In the Cline chat box, you will give prompts from this guide Step 4: Before any prompt, add this at the top: @docs/06_ImplementationPlan.docx @docs/05_BackendSchema.docx (@ mentions tell Cline to read those documents) Step 5: Paste the prompt from this guide Step 6: Cline will write files directly into your project Step 7: Review what Cline wrote, run it, verify it works ΓÜá Gemini 1.5 Pro free tier: 1500 requests/day, 1M token context. More than enough for daily tasks. If you reach the limit, switch to Claude free tier for that day, or continue tomorrow. PART 2: TEAM ROLES ΓÇö 5 MEMBERS Every team member has a specific role. Return {content, metadata, chunk_index}. embed_chunks(chunks) ΓåÆ embeds each chunk with EmbeddingService.embed_passage() ΓÇö MUST use &apos;passage: &apos; prefix. store_chunks(document_id, chunks, embeddings) ΓåÆ bulk INSERT into knowledge_documents and knowledge_chunks tables. Create scripts/seed_knowledge.py: reads scripts/seed_urls.json, runs pipeline for each URL, prints progress per document. Create scripts/seed_urls.json with 10 real ICAR/KAU URLs for rice and maize documents. Show ingestion.py in full. Your Task 4: Scheme + Market Data (Phase 3) New Cline session: Agri-SAGE. Read @docs/05_AgriSAGE_BackendSchema.docx Sections 1.9 and @docs/06_AgriSAGE_ImplementationPlan.docx Task 3.3. Create scripts/seed_schemes.py: Seeds the schemes table with real data for: 1. PMFBY (Pradhan Mantri Fasal Bima Yojana): central scheme, all India, crops=[rice,maize,tomato,banana], eligibility_rules={land_ownership_type:[owner,tenant], aadhaar_required:true, enrollment_window_days_after_sowing:max_30}, benefits={insurance_sum_insured_per_ha:unknown_varies_by_state, premium_pct_kharif:2.0, premium_pct_rabi:1.5}, valid_until=2027-03-31, enrollment_url=https://pmfby.gov.in 2. PM-KISAN: central scheme, all India, crops=null (all crops), eligibility_rules={land_ha_max:2, not_income_taxpayer:true, not_government_employee:true}, benefits={annual_amount_inr:6000, paid_in:3_installments}, valid_until=2028-03-31 3. At least 3 Kerala state agriculture department schemes ΓÇö find real ones from keralaagriculturedept.gov.in Create backend/agents/market.py: fetches e-NAM data from data.gov.in API resource_id=9ef84268-d588-465a-a308-a864a43d0070. Filter by state+district. Store in market_prices table. Compute nearest_markets using haversine formula. Cache 30 min in-memory. Show seed_schemes.py in full. Same prompts work. GitHub Copilot completions used up Use Cline with Gemini instead for file generation. Copilot is supplementary. Claude free tier messages used Switch back to Gemini via Cline. Paste relevant spec section. AI generates too much at once Break task into smaller steps. Give one function at a time. Context window too large for free model Split documents ΓÇö only attach the specific section needed, not the whole document. PART 1: SETUP ΓÇö BEFORE YOU GIVE ANYTHING TO AI 1.1 Which AI Tool to Use Tool Best For Cost Recommendation Cursor IDE Full project build ΓÇö reads entire codebase, writes files directly, understands project context $20/month (free tier available) BEST CHOICE ΓÇö purpose-built for AI coding. Reads all your docs as context. VSCode + GitHub Copilot Writing code with AI assistance, good for experienced developers $10/month Good ΓÇö but requires more manual context feeding per session. Claude Projects (claude.ai) Planning, architecture, writing complex code blocks, reviewing Included in Pro plan Use alongside Cursor for complex decisions. Not for direct file writing. ChatGPT Projects Similar to Claude Projects ChatGPT Plus Alternative to Claude Projects. Use for planning/review only. ΓÜá Do NOT try to build the entire project in one AI chat session. AI models have context limits. Build phase by phase, task by task. Each task is one AI session. 1.2 Cursor IDE Setup ΓÇö Recommended Cursor is the best tool for this project. PHASE 1: VISION MVP ΓÇö Weeks 3-8 Prompt P1.1 ΓÇö Auth Endpoints New session. Attach: TRD + Security doc + Backend Schema. Agri-SAGE Phase 1 ΓÇö create the complete authentication system. Create backend/api/v1/auth.py with these 4 endpoints following Security document Section 2 exactly: POST /api/v1/auth/send-otp: accept {phone: str}, validate E.164 format (+91XXXXXXXXXX), generate 6-digit OTP using secrets.randbelow(1000000) zero-padded, hash with bcrypt rounds=10, store in otp_sessions table with expires_at=now+300s, check rate limit (max 5 per phone per 10 minutes ΓÇö query otp_sessions count), in DEVELOPMENT environment print OTP to console and return {message: OTP sent, expires_in: 300}. In production call MSG91 API. POST /api/v1/auth/verify-otp: accept {phone, otp}, find most recent unused unexpired otp_session for phone, check attempts&lt;5, verify bcrypt hash, mark used=True, create or get user record, generate JWT access token (15min HS256) and refresh token (SHA256 hash stored, 7 days), return {access_token, refresh_token, user: {id, phone, name, language, subscription_tier}}. POST /api/v1/auth/refresh: accept {refresh_token}, hash it, find in refresh_tokens not revoked not expired, revoke old, issue new pair. POST /api/v1/auth/logout: accept {refresh_token}, mark revoked=True. Create backend/dependencies.py: require_auth dependency that decodes JWT, validates, sets PostgreSQL session variable SET LOCAL app.current_user_id = user_id for RLS, returns payload dict. PART 3: HOW TO HANDLE ERRORS 3.1 When AI Produces Wrong Code Do NOT start over. Give a targeted correction prompt: [Context: Building Agri-SAGE. Previous task: [describe task]. Problem: [describe exactly what is wrong]. Fix ONLY this specific issue: [describe issue]. Do not change anything else. Show only the changed section of the file. Example: Context: Building Agri-SAGE vision agent. Problem: The VisionAgent is calling the LLM to get disease names instead of using disease_classes.json. Fix ONLY this: Replace the LLM call with loading class names from backend/data/disease_classes.json at initialization. Do not change anything else in vision.py. 3.2 When AI Forgets Context AI models sometimes forget earlier decisions. Use this reset prompt: Agri-SAGE context refresh: - Stack: FastAPI + PostgreSQL + pgvector + LangGraph + Pydantic v2 + ONNX Runtime - Python 3.11, SQLAlchemy 2.0 async - Android: Kotlin + Jetpack Compose + Room + WorkManager - Primary language: Malayalam for farmers - CRITICAL: LLM only synthesizes ΓÇö never invents facts, chemicals, or scheme eligibility - All simulation outputs labelled &apos;modelled estimate&apos; - Confidence states: CONFIRMED/LIKELY/POSSIBLE/UNCERTAIN/INSUFFICIENT_DATA/CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE/OUT_OF_SCOPE Now: [your task] 3.3 When AI Adds Unwanted Features STOP. You are adding [feature name] which is NOT in scope for this phase. Agri-SAGE out-of-scope items: social network, blockchain, drone, 50+ crops, autonomous purchasing, yield guarantees. Remove [feature name] and implement only what was asked: [restate original task]. 3.4 When Tests Fail Agri-SAGE test failure. Test: [test name]. Error: [paste exact error message]. Do not change the test ΓÇö the tests are correct. Fix the implementation. The test is checking: [explain what test checks]. The expected behavior per the specification is: [describe expected behavior]. Fix only the implementation code to make this test pass. 3.5 When AI Suggests Using a Paid API STOP. Agri-SAGE must be built at zero cost. [API name] is not in our approved free stack. Our approved stack: FastAPI, PostgreSQL, pgvector, LangGraph, PyTorch, ONNX Runtime, Qwen3 via Ollama, multilingual-e5-large, DSSAT, Open-Meteo (dev), Cloudflare R2, Oracle Always-Free, Firebase FCM free tier, Razorpay (2% per transaction only on revenue). Implement this using only the approved stack. Alternative approach: [suggest alternative if you know one]. PART 4: DAILY BUILD WORKFLOW 4.1 Your Daily Routine While Building Step Action Time 1. (30 seconds) 2. Explain: &apos;This is the farmer&apos;s daily view ΓÇö morning briefing, quick actions, offline indicator.&apos; (30 seconds) 3. Tap &apos;Check Crop&apos;. Camera opens. Show quality indicator turning green. (30 seconds) 4. Take photo of a leaf (real plant). Show advisory loading screen with progress steps. (30 seconds) 5. Show advisory result: confidence badge, evidence section, recommended action, modelled estimate label. (60 seconds) 6. Tap voice button ΓÇö advisory reads aloud in Malayalam. (20 seconds) 7. Show offline mode: turn on airplane mode, take another photo ΓÇö advisory still works. (30 seconds) 8. Show government scheme card: &apos;PMFBY scheme available for this farmer.&apos; (20 seconds) 9. Close with: &apos;This is Agri-SAGE ΓÇö agricultural decision intelligence for Kerala farmers, built entirely free, offline-capable, in their language.&apos; (30 seconds) Total demo time: approximately 5 minutes. README.md SECTION 3: GIT WORKFLOW FOR 5 MEMBERS 3.1 Branch Strategy ΓÇö Simple and Safe Every member works on their own branch. Never commit directly to main. Team Lead merges everything. Branch Name Owner What Gets Committed Here main Member 1 merges here Only tested, working code. Never commit directly. dev/m1-backend Member 1 Database, auth, farm CRUD, deployment dev/m2-agents Member 2 All AI agents, LangGraph, LLM service dev/m3-android Member 3 All Android screens, Room DB, WorkManager dev/m4-data Member 4 JSON data files, vision model, knowledge ingestion dev/m5-tests Member 5 All test files, CI/CD, security audit scripts 3.2 Daily Git Commands ΓÇö What Every Member Does Morning ΓÇö start of every work day: # Get latest changes from main git checkout main git pull origin main git checkout dev/YOUR-BRANCH git merge main # This keeps your branch up to date with others&apos; merged work During the day ΓÇö after completing each task: # See what you changed git status # Stage your changes git add -A # Commit with clear message git commit -m &apos;M2: implement critic agent conflict detection rules&apos; # Format: M[your number]: [what you built] # Examples: # M1: add JWT auth endpoints with bcrypt OTP hashing # M3: create camera screen with quality indicator # M4: create disease_classes.json with all 22 ONNX classes # M5: add critic safety unit tests # Push to your branch git push origin dev/YOUR-BRANCH When a task is complete and you want it merged to main: # 1. Push your branch git push origin dev/YOUR-BRANCH # 2. Go to GitHub website ΓåÆ Your repo # 3. You will see a yellow banner: &apos;dev/YOUR-BRANCH had recent pushes&apos; # 4. Click: &apos;Compare &amp; pull request&apos; # 5. Title: &apos;M[number]: [what you built]&apos; # 6. Description: list what files you created/changed # 7. Click: &apos;Create pull request&apos; # 8. Message Member 1 on WhatsApp: &apos;PR ready for review&apos; # Member 1 reviews, runs tests, and merges if everything passes 3.3 What Member 1 Does to Merge # Member 1 ΓÇö reviewing and merging a PR # 1. Go to GitHub ΓåÆ Pull Requests ΓåÆ click the PR # 2. Look at &apos;Files changed&apos; tab ΓÇö read through the code # 3. Check: does this match the specification document? # 4. On your computer: git checkout main git pull origin main git checkout dev/THEIR-BRANCH git pull origin dev/THEIR-BRANCH # 5. Run tests cd agrisage docker compose up -d pytest tests/ -v --tb=short # 6. If tests pass: go back to GitHub PR ΓåÆ &apos;Merge pull request&apos; ΓåÆ Confirm # 7. If tests fail: comment on PR with exact error. Do not merge. 3.4 Handling Merge Conflicts A merge conflict happens when two members edit the same file. Here is how to fix it: # Git will show: CONFLICT (content): Merge conflict in backend/main.py # Open the conflicted file in VSCode # You will see markers like this: &lt;&lt;&lt;&lt;&lt;&lt;&lt; HEAD your version of the code ======= their version of the code &gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt;&gt; dev/m1-backend # In VSCode: click &apos;Accept Current Change&apos; or &apos;Accept Incoming Change&apos; # OR manually edit to keep the correct version # Then: git add backend/main.py git commit -m &apos;Resolve merge conflict in main.py&apos; ΓÜá If you are unsure which version to keep in a conflict ΓÇö do NOT guess. Message the other person on WhatsApp and resolve together. A wrong merge can break the project for everyone. Install with all defaults. 10 min Open terminal, type &apos;git --version&apos;, see a version number 9 Configure Git: git config --global user.name &apos;YourName&apos; and git config --global user.email &apos;you@email.com&apos; 2 min git config --list shows your name and email 10 Accept GitHub repo invite from Member 1. Clone repo: git clone [url]. cd agrisage. code . 15 min VSCode opens with agrisage folder, docs/ folder visible 11 Verify Cline works: Click Cline icon ΓåÆ Type &apos;Hello, list the files in my project&apos; ΓåÆ Cline should respond 5 min Cline responds and lists your project files 4.2 How to Use Cline Effectively ΓÇö For Beginners Cline is your main coding tool. Here is how to use it step by step: Step 1: Click the Cline robot icon in the left sidebar of VSCode Step 2: A chat panel opens on the right side Step 3: At the top of the chat, type @ to attach documents Example: @docs/05_BackendSchema.docx This tells Cline to read that document before answering Step 4: After the @ mention, paste your prompt from the build guide Step 5: Press Enter ΓÇö Cline reads the document and writes code Step 6: Cline will ask &apos;Do you want to create this file?&apos; ΓåÆ Click Yes Step 7: The file appears in your project automatically Step 8: Review what Cline created ΓÇö read through it Step 9: If something looks wrong, use the correction prompt from build guide Part 9 Γ£ô Cline with Gemini 1.5 Pro has a 1 million token context window. This means it can read MULTIPLE documents at once. For complex tasks, attach 2-3 documents. For simple tasks, attach just 1. ΓÜá Never blindly run code you have not read. Even if Cline writes it, you must read it. If you do not understand something, ask Cline: &apos;Explain what this function does in simple terms.&apos; 4.3 When You Hit Free Tier Limits Tool Daily Limit What To Do When Limit Hit Gemini API via Cline 1,500 requests/day free tier Switch to Claude at claude.ai for that day. Same prompts work. Tomorrow reset continues. GitHub Copilot free 2,000 completions/month Use Cline for file generation. Copilot is for inline suggestions only. Claude.ai free ~30 messages/day Switch to Gemini via Cline. They alternate well. Google AI Studio direct Very generous ΓÇö rarely hit If hit: wait a few hours, usually resets SECTION 5: MEMBER-BY-MEMBER STEP-BY-STEP GUIDE 5.1 MEMBER 1 ΓÇö Team Lead &amp; Backend ΓÇö Day-by-Day You start first. === 1_AgriSAGE_PRD.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE PRODUCT REQUIREMENTS DOCUMENT (PRD) Version 1.0 ΓÇö Production-Ready Specification Scalable Agricultural Guidance Engine Field Detail Document Type Product Requirements Document (PRD) Project Agri-SAGE ΓÇö Scalable Agricultural Guidance Engine Version 1.0 Status Final ΓÇö Implementation Ready Target Platform Android (Primary) + Web Dashboard Primary Market Smallholder Farmers, Kerala ΓåÆ Pan-India ΓåÆ Global Build Cost Zero ΓÇö Fully free/open-source stack Revenue Model Freemium Subscription post-launch Architecture Offline-first, Multi-agent AI, Physics-based Simulation 1. EXECUTIVE SUMMARY 1.1 Product Vision Agri-SAGE is a farm decision intelligence platform ΓÇö not a chatbot, not a disease detector, not a weather app. It is all of these working as one unified system: the first offline-first, simulation-grounded, evidence-transparent agricultural decision engine built for smallholder farmers in India. Every farmer in India who cannot afford an agronomist, who has no reliable internet, who communicates in Malayalam or Tamil or Telugu, who farms 1 hectare of paddy and needs to know if the yellowing on his lower leaves is blast disease or nitrogen deficiency ΓÇö and what the rain forecast means for when to spray ΓÇö and whether a government scheme covers the treatment ΓÇö that farmer is our user. 1.2 The Problem Being Solved A smallholder farmer today needs to answer 20+ interacting questions to make a single crop management decision. These questions are currently fragmented across agronomists, government helplines, weather apps, WhatsApp groups, YouTube, local shops, and personal experience. Each source gives partial, often conflicting information. No single system connects them. The cost of wrong decisions: crop loss, wasted inputs, delayed treatment, missed government support, unnecessary pesticide spend. For a family farming 2 hectares, one wrong decision in a season can mean 20-40% income loss. 1.3 The Solution Agri-SAGE combines: multimodal input (image + voice + location + farm history) ΓåÆ multi-agent AI analysis (14 specialized agents) ΓåÆ physics-based crop simulation (DSSAT) ΓåÆ evidence-transparent advisory ΓåÆ proactive daily monitoring ΓåÆ government scheme matching ΓåÆ economic scenario comparison ΓÇö delivered in regional language, offline-capable, on a Rs.8,000 Android phone. 1.4 Key Success Metrics Metric Definition Year 1 Target Year 3 Target Monthly Active Farmers Farmers who generate ΓëÑ1 advisory per month 10,000 500,000 Daily Active Rate MAF who open app daily 30% 55% Advisory Acceptance Rate Farmer marks &apos;I will try this&apos; 55% 70% Outcome Verified Rate Farmer reports outcome after intervention 25% 45% Expert Agreement Rate Agronomist panel agrees with advisory 80% 90% Offline Usage Rate Sessions started without internet 40% 50% Subscription Conversion Free ΓåÆ paid after 60-day trial 8% 18% Farmer Retention (90-day) Active at day 90 after registration 35% 55% 2. PRODUCT SCOPE 3.1 In-Scope: MVP (Phase 1-2, Months 1-8) Android application (minimum Android 8.0 / API 26) Farmer registration via OTP (phone number only ΓÇö no password) Farm profile: location, field, crop, cultivar, planting date Image capture with real-time quality check and retry guidance Vision-based disease/pest/deficiency detection (5 launch crops) Weather integration with agricultural indicator derivation Crop stage estimation via GDD (Growing Degree Days) Knowledge RAG: ICAR + KAU documents for 5 crops Critic agent: cross-validation and confidence calibration Advisory output: diagnosis + evidence + action + cost estimate + sources Advisory history and farm observation timeline Offline vision inference (ONNX on-device model) Offline knowledge cache for supported crops Malayalam language support (text + voice output) Morning briefing: daily farm status card Backend API (FastAPI + PostgreSQL + pgvector) 3.2 In-Scope: V2 (Phase 3-4, Months 9-18) Government scheme matching (PMFBY, PM-KISAN, RKVY) Simulation engine: DSSAT scenarios for rice and maize A/B/C scenario comparison with economic analysis Voice input in Malayalam (Bhashini STT) Proactive risk alerts (push notifications) Intervention tracking and outcome recording Expert review dashboard (agronomist interface) Tamil, Telugu, Hindi language support Season summary report 3.3 In-Scope: V3 (Phase 5-6, Months 19-30) Market price integration (e-NAM API) Economics agent: full cost-profit analysis Farmer constraint optimization (budget, labor, water) Digital farm twin (seasonal learning and memory) FPO / group advisory dashboard Web application for extension workers and agronomists Satellite imagery integration (NDVI alerts) Multi-crop expansion (target: 15 crops) 3.4 Explicitly Out of Scope (Never Build) Social network or community feed (misinformation risk) Blockchain of any kind Drone integration Autonomous purchasing or chemical ordering Yield guarantees or financial return promises Custom foundation model training from scratch 50+ crops at launch (quality &gt; quantity) 4. FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS 4.1 FR-001: Farmer Onboarding ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-001-01 Phone OTP registration ΓÇö no email, no password required P0 Farmer registers with phone number + 6-digit OTP in under 90 seconds FR-001-02 Language selection on first launch: Malayalam, Tamil, Telugu, Hindi, English P0 All subsequent UI and voice in selected language FR-001-03 Farm setup wizard: name, approximate location, field area, crop, planting date P0 Setup completable in under 3 minutes without typing ΓÇö use dropdowns and map pin FR-001-04 Cultivar selection from regional list (pre-populated by state) P1 Jyothi, Uma, Kanchana etc. shown for Kerala rice FR-001-05 Guest mode: allow advisory without account (advisory not saved) P2 Farmer can scan crop and get advisory without registering 4.2 FR-002: Image Capture &amp; Quality ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-002-01 Real-time image quality indicator during camera preview P0 Green/yellow/red indicator updates within 500ms as camera moves FR-002-02 Blur detection before accepting image P0 Laplacian variance &lt; 100 ΓåÆ reject with message &apos;Photo is blurry ΓÇö move closer&apos; FR-002-03 Lighting check before accepting image P0 Too dark or too bright ΓåÆ reject with specific instruction FR-002-04 Crop region visibility check P0 &lt; 30% crop in frame ΓåÆ &apos;Please fill the frame with the affected plant area&apos; FR-002-05 Multiple image capture (up to 5 per observation) P1 Farmer can add multiple angles ΓÇö improves diagnostic confidence FR-002-06 Image compression before upload (max 800px longest edge) P0 Reduces mobile data usage without losing diagnostic information FR-002-07 Non-crop image rejection (OOD detection) P0 Hand/wall/random object ΓåÆ &apos;This does not appear to be a crop ΓÇö please retake&apos; 4.3 FR-003: Advisory Generation ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-003-01 Advisory delivered within 8 seconds for standard path P0 P95 latency &lt; 8s for online mode; &lt; 3s for offline vision-only FR-003-02 Advisory must include: diagnosis, confidence state, risk level, recommended action, timing, cost estimate, evidence summary, sources P0 All 8 fields present in every advisory response FR-003-03 Confidence state must be one of 7 defined states ΓÇö not a percentage alone P0 CONFIRMED / LIKELY / POSSIBLE / UNCERTAIN / INSUFFICIENT_DATA / CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE / OUT_OF_SCOPE FR-003-04 Evidence must list contributing factors visibly P0 &apos;Image matched blast pattern + humidity 3 consecutive wet days + tillering stage (susceptible)&apos; FR-003-05 &apos;What would change this?&apos; section required in every advisory P0 At least 1 factor shown e.g. &apos;A soil test would help rule out nitrogen deficiency&apos; FR-003-06 Source citation with authority tier required P0 &apos;Based on: ICAR Rice Disease Advisory 2024 (Tier 1)&apos; FR-003-07 Chemical recommendations must trace to verified CIB&amp;RC record P0 Safety check blocks advisory if product not found in label database FR-003-08 All simulation outputs labelled &apos;Modelled estimate&apos; P0 Never shown as guaranteed result 4.4 FR-004: Offline Mode ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-004-01 App opens and shows last farm state without internet P0 Home screen loads in &lt; 2 seconds offline using cached data FR-004-02 Vision inference runs fully on-device without internet P0 ONNX model inference completes in &lt; 4 seconds on Snapdragon 665 FR-004-03 Cached knowledge used for advisory when offline P0 Regional crop advisory shown with &apos;Cached ΓÇö last updated [date]&apos; label FR-004-04 All farm actions queued locally when offline P0 Observations, outcomes, feedback stored locally and synced on reconnect FR-004-05 Offline mode clearly indicated to farmer P0 Banner: &apos;Working offline ΓÇö live weather unavailable. Saved data shown.&apos; FR-004-06 Stale data clearly labelled ΓÇö never shown as current P0 Weather cached &gt; 6hrs shows &apos;Weather data from [time] ΓÇö may not be current&apos; 4.5 FR-005: Daily Intelligence ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-005-01 Morning briefing generated daily before 7am for each active farmer P1 Briefing covers: crop stage, top risk today, weather summary, one recommended action FR-005-02 Proactive risk alert when disease favorability index exceeds threshold P1 Push notification with crop-specific context ΓÇö not generic FR-005-03 Spray window alert: &apos;Good spray conditions today 7-10am&apos; P1 Alert generated when rain prob &lt; 20% for 4hr window AND wind &lt; 15 km/h FR-005-04 Crop stage milestone notification P1 &apos;Your paddy is entering flowering stage ΓÇö elevated blast risk. What to check:&apos; FR-005-05 Market price alert when significant change (&gt;10%) P2 Notification includes current price, trend, nearest market 4.6 FR-006: Government Scheme Matching ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-006-01 PMFBY scheme matching based on farmer profile and crop P1 Shows applicable insurance scheme with enrollment steps and deadline FR-006-02 PM-KISAN eligibility check P1 Shows eligibility criteria with farmer&apos;s matching status FR-006-03 State-specific scheme database (Kerala Agriculture Department) P1 At least 10 active schemes at launch with eligibility rules FR-006-04 Scheme advisory always uses cautious language P0 &apos;You appear to meet these criteria ΓÇö final eligibility determined by relevant authority&apos; FR-006-05 Scheme document freshness check ΓÇö expired schemes flagged P0 Schemes past valid_until date shown with &apos;Please verify with local authority&apos; 4.7 FR-007: Voice Interface ID Requirement Priority Acceptance Criteria FR-007-01 Voice input for crop problem description in Malayalam P1 Farmer can say &apos;α┤Äα┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡å α┤¿α╡åα┤▓α╡ìα┤▓α┤┐α╡╜ α┤çα┤Öα╡ìα┤Öα┤¿α╡å α┤¬α┤╛α┤ƒα╡üα┤òα╡╛&apos; and get advisory FR-007-02 Voice output for all advisories (TTS) P1 Any screen has &apos;Listen&apos; button ΓÇö full advisory read in selected language FR-007-03 On-device STT fallback when internet unavailable P1 Android SpeechRecognizer used when Bhashini API unreachable FR-007-04 Voice input is parsed for structured intent ΓÇö not passed raw to LLM P0 Intent extraction layer processes transcript before any LLM call 5. NON-FUNCTIONAL REQUIREMENTS 5.1 Performance Requirement Target Measurement App cold start &lt; 2.5 seconds on Tier 2 device Android profiler ΓÇö Redmi 10A baseline Online advisory generation &lt; 8 seconds P95 Server-side timing from POST /diagnose Offline vision inference &lt; 4 seconds on Snapdragon 665 On-device benchmark Offline app home screen load &lt; 2 seconds Cold start with cached data Image upload (800px, 4G) &lt; 5 seconds Network timing test Weather data freshness &lt; 30 minutes Cache TTL monitoring Knowledge cache update Background, non-blocking WorkManager job ΓÇö never interrupts user Sync after reconnect &lt; 60 seconds for queued observations Sync engine timing test 5.2 Reliability Requirement Target API availability 99.5% monthly uptime (free-tier infrastructure ΓÇö honest target) Vision model accuracy (field) Macro-F1 &gt; 0.82 on field test set ΓÇö measured before every deployment Advisory safety check pass rate 100% ΓÇö zero unsafe recommendations reach farmer Data loss tolerance Zero ΓÇö all observations persisted locally before API call Crash rate &lt; 0.5% sessions on supported devices Offline functionality 100% of core features work without internet 5.3 Security &amp; Privacy Requirement Implementation Authentication OTP only ΓÇö no password storage ΓÇö JWT 15min access + 7day refresh Data encryption at rest PostgreSQL column encryption for sensitive fields; SQLite encrypted on Android Data encryption in transit TLS 1.3 minimum ΓÇö certificate pinning on Android Row-level security PostgreSQL RLS ΓÇö users see only their own data ΓÇö enforced at DB level Image privacy User can delete all images ΓÇö storage purged within 24 hours Location precision Internal: GPS coordinates. External/analytics: district-level only Data export User can export all their data as JSON on request Account deletion Full data deletion within 30 days of request Audit trail All advisory generations, data exports, account changes ΓÇö immutable log Minimum data collection Only what is strictly needed for stated feature ΓÇö no passive tracking 5.4 Accessibility &amp; Localization Requirement Target Languages at launch Malayalam, English (Tamil, Telugu, Hindi in V2) Voice support All languages ΓÇö input and output Minimum touch target 48dp ├ù 48dp per Material Design 3 guidelines Text size Minimum 16sp body text ΓÇö farmer-friendly High contrast mode Supported ΓÇö green brand palette maintains WCAG AA contrast Literacy accommodation Every text element has voice alternative ΓÇö app usable by low-literacy users Screen reader TalkBack compatibility verified on all primary screens 6. SUBSCRIPTION &amp; MONETIZATION MODEL 6.1 Pricing Tiers Tier Price Features Target User Free (Freemium) Rs.0 forever 5 advisories/month, basic weather, crop stage, 1 farm, Malayalam only Evaluation ΓÇö first 60 days unlimited Kisan Basic Rs.49/month or Rs.449/year Unlimited advisories, offline vision, morning briefing, alerts, 2 farms, all languages Individual smallholder farmer Kisan Pro Rs.149/month or Rs.1,299/year Everything in Basic + simulation scenarios, scheme matching, market prices, economic analysis, 5 farms, priority support Progressive farmer, FPO member Agronomist Rs.499/month Everything in Pro + expert dashboard, 500 farmer profiles, group advisory, API access Agricultural extension worker, agronomist FPO/Enterprise Custom pricing Unlimited farmers, white-label option, custom knowledge base, dedicated support, data export Farmer Producer Organizations, NGOs, Government 6.2 Free-to-Paid Conversion Strategy 60-day free trial with full Pro features ΓÇö no credit card required Trial-end: soft paywall ΓÇö advisory still shown but blurred until subscription First payment: UPI-native ΓÇö no credit card needed (PhonePe, GPay, Paytm) Kisan Credit Card holders: special partnership pricing (Rs.29/month) FPO group plans: FPO pays for all member farmers ΓÇö Rs.25/farmer/month at scale Government subsidy path: partner with state agriculture departments for subsidized access 6.3 Revenue Projection (Conservative) Year MAF Paying Users ARPU/Year ARR Year 1 10,000 800 (8%) Rs.600 Rs.4.8 lakh Year 2 75,000 9,000 (12%) Rs.750 Rs.67.5 lakh Year 3 500,000 90,000 (18%) Rs.900 Rs.8.1 crore Year 5 3,000,000 600,000 (20%) Rs.1,100 Rs.66 crore ΓÜá These are projections for planning purposes only. Actual revenue depends on product-market fit, retention, and execution. Do not commit to these numbers with investors or partners. 7. RISKS &amp; MITIGATIONS Risk Probability Impact Mitigation Vision model performs poorly on field images (domain gap from lab datasets) HIGH HIGH Collect 500+ field images per crop BEFORE model deployment. Separate lab and field accuracy metrics. Never report lab accuracy as field accuracy. Open-Meteo API non-commercial restriction blocks commercial launch HIGH HIGH Build WeatherProvider abstraction from day 1. IMD commercial API as V2 replacement. Budget for commercial weather API before monetization. APSIM commercial license required for revenue deployment HIGH HIGH Use DSSAT (BSD-3-Clause) as primary simulation backend. APSIM used only for research/validation. Abstraction layer already designed. Bhashini API approval delay (4-6 weeks) MEDIUM MEDIUM Apply for access in Month 1. Build Android SpeechRecognizer fallback as default. Bhashini enables quality upgrade, not core dependency. Hallucination: LLM invents pesticide dosage LOW CRITICAL All chemical recommendations must trace to CIB&amp;RC label database. LLM cannot output chemical name not in database. Critic agent blocks unsafe advisories. Free-tier infrastructure insufficient at scale MEDIUM MEDIUM Architecture designed on Oracle Always-Free during pilot. Migration path to VPS/cloud documented. Revenue model covers infrastructure at scale. Low farmer retention after 60-day trial MEDIUM HIGH Morning briefing and proactive alerts drive daily habit before paywall. Measure retention weekly from week 1. PMFBY/scheme data becomes stale MEDIUM HIGH Document freshness system with valid_until dates. Expired docs flagged ΓÇö never served as current. Monthly automated refresh from official sources. Model drift: accuracy degrades as pests/diseases evolve LOW HIGH Monthly model evaluation on golden test set. Outcome data used to identify new patterns. Annual model retraining minimum. 9. ACCEPTANCE CRITERIA &amp; LAUNCH READINESS 9.1 MVP Launch Checklist ΓÇö All Must Pass Vision model: Macro-F1 &gt; 0.82 on field test set (farmer-level split, not lab split) Vision model: ECE (Expected Calibration Error) &lt; 0.12 after temperature scaling Advisory safety audit: 100 random advisories reviewed by 2 agronomists ΓÇö zero unsafe recommendations Critic agent: All 8 safety checks verified with test cases Offline mode: Full advisory generated without internet on Tier 2 device Performance: Advisory P95 &lt; 8 seconds on production server Performance: App cold start &lt; 2.5 seconds on Redmi 10A (Tier 2 baseline) Security: OWASP Mobile Top 10 checklist completed Malayalam: All primary screens reviewed by native Malayalam speaker Government schemes: All scheme docs have valid_until dates and source URLs License registry: All dependencies verified ΓÇö no unlicensed commercial use Closed beta: 50 farmers tested app for 2+ weeks with positive NPS 9.2 Definition of Done per Feature A feature is DONE when: Unit tests pass (&gt;80% coverage for business logic) Integration test with real API data passes Tested on Tier 1, Tier 2, and Tier 3 devices Tested in offline mode Malayalam text reviewed by native speaker Safety check (if applicable) passes agronomist review Performance target met Accessibility: minimum TalkBack navigation verified 10. PRODUCT ROADMAP SUMMARY Phase Timeline Key Deliverables User Value Phase 0: Foundation Months 1-2 Repo structure, DB schema, auth, farm CRUD, CI/CD No user value yet ΓÇö internal infrastructure Phase 1: Vision MVP Months 2-4 Vision model, image quality, basic advisory, offline inference Farmer can get disease diagnosis from photo ΓÇö basic but real Phase 2: Context &amp; Knowledge Months 4-8 Weather agent, crop stage, RAG, Critic, full advisory, Malayalam Full multi-signal advisory in Malayalam ΓÇö core product Phase 3: Intelligence Months 8-14 Simulation, scenarios, schemes, voice input, morning briefing, alerts Proactive intelligence ΓÇö daily use habit forms Phase 4: Economics Months 14-20 Market prices, cost-profit analysis, farmer constraints, season report Full economic decision support ΓÇö differentiated product Phase 5: Scale &amp; Validation Months 20-30 Expert network, field validation study, multi-state, web dashboard Scientific credibility + B2B opportunities Phase 6: Global Months 30+ International localization, satellite integration, API marketplace Platform business ΓÇö not just an app AGRI-SAGE PRD v1.0 ΓÇö CONFIDENTIAL &amp; IMPLEMENTATION-READY Recommend ΓåÆ Act ΓåÆ Observe ΓåÆ Learn ΓåÆ Improve

AGRI-SAGE TECHNICAL REQUIREMENTS DOCUMENT (TRD) Version 1.0 ΓÇö Full Stack Technical Specification Field Detail Document Technical Requirements Document (TRD) Audience Lead Developer, VSCode AI Agent, Technical Reviewers Stack Python FastAPI ┬╖ PostgreSQL ┬╖ pgvector ┬╖ LangGraph ┬╖ PyTorch ┬╖ ONNX ┬╖ Android/Kotlin Build Cost Zero ΓÇö 100% free/open-source stack Deployment Target Oracle Always-Free ΓåÆ Budget VPS ΓåÆ Cloud (as revenue permits) 1. SYSTEM ARCHITECTURE OVERVIEW 1.1 Architecture Principles Offline-first: Every core feature must function without internet. Internet enhances, not enables. Provider-independent: No hard dependency on any paid API. Every external service behind an abstraction interface. Deterministic-first: Rules and cache answer before LLM is called. LLM synthesizes, does not invent. Evidence-grounded: Every advisory output must carry a traceable evidence bundle ΓÇö no free-floating AI assertions. Schema-first: All agent outputs are validated JSON schemas ΓÇö never free-form text passed between agents. Cost-aware: Architecture designed for zero infrastructure cost during development and minimum cost during pilot. Security-by-default: Row-level security, JWT auth, encrypted storage, input validation ΓÇö from day one. 1.2 High-Level Component Map Layer Component Technology Purpose Mobile Android App Kotlin + Jetpack Compose Primary farmer interface Mobile Local ML Runtime ONNX Runtime Android On-device vision inference Mobile Local Database Room (SQLite) Offline data persistence Mobile Background Sync WorkManager Async sync when connected Mobile Voice Bhashini API + Android STT fallback Speech input/output API Gateway FastAPI + nginx Routing, rate limiting, auth API Auth Service FastAPI + JWT + OTP Phone-based authentication API Farm Service FastAPI + SQLAlchemy Farm/field/crop CRUD API Advisory Service FastAPI + LangGraph Multi-agent orchestration API Vision Service FastAPI + ONNX Runtime Server-side image inference API Weather Service FastAPI + httpx Weather abstraction + caching API Knowledge Service FastAPI + pgvector RAG retrieval + doc management API Scheme Service FastAPI + SQLAlchemy Government scheme matching API Simulation Service FastAPI + subprocess DSSAT execution + results API Notification Service FastAPI + FCM Push alerts + morning briefing API Sync Service FastAPI Mobile offline sync coordination Data Primary DB PostgreSQL 16 All relational data Data Vector Index pgvector extension Knowledge embeddings Data Object Storage Cloudflare R2 Images, reports, model files Data Cache In-memory (FastAPI) ΓåÆ Redis v2+ Weather, session, hot queries AI Vision Model EfficientNet-B3 (ONNX INT8) Disease/pest/deficiency detection AI LLM Server Qwen3-7B (4-bit) Advisory synthesis AI LLM On-device Qwen3-1.7B (ONNX) On-device language generation AI Embeddings multilingual-e5-large Knowledge retrieval embeddings AI Reranker cross-encoder-ms-marco-MiniLM RAG result reranking Simulation Primary DSSAT CSM (BSD-3-Clause) Crop scenario simulation Simulation Research APSIM Next Gen Validation only ΓÇö NOT production commercial Simulation Offline Local rules engine Simplified offline scenarios 2. BACKEND TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION 2.1 Environment &amp; Runtime Component Specification Notes Python version 3.11+ Required for modern asyncio and type hints FastAPI 0.110+ Async-native, auto OpenAPI docs SQLAlchemy 2.0+ async Async engine required ΓÇö not legacy 1.x Alembic Latest Database migration management PostgreSQL 16+ pgvector requires PG 12+ but 16 recommended pgvector 0.6+ Approximate nearest neighbor search for RAG LangGraph 0.2+ Stateful multi-agent workflow orchestration PyTorch 2.0+ Vision model training and server inference ONNX Runtime 1.17+ Cross-platform inference Pydantic 2.0+ Schema validation ΓÇö required for agent I/O contracts httpx Latest Async HTTP client for external API calls Structlog Latest Structured JSON logging pytest Latest + pytest-asyncio All tests async-compatible Docker Latest Container runtime Docker Compose Latest Local development orchestration 2.2 Project Directory Structure agrisage/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ backend/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ main.py # DATABASE TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION 3.1 PostgreSQL Configuration -- Required PostgreSQL extensions CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgis; -- Geographic queries CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector; -- pgvector for embeddings CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pg_cron; -- Scheduled freshness checks CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS uuid-ossp; -- UUID generation -- Performance settings (for free-tier 1GB RAM instance) shared_buffers = 256MB effective_cache_size = 768MB maintenance_work_mem = 64MB work_mem = 4MB max_connections = 100 3.2 Complete Database Schema Users &amp; Authentication CREATE TABLE users ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), phone VARCHAR(15) UNIQUE NOT NULL, name VARCHAR(100), language VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;ml&apos;, -- BCP-47 location_lat DECIMAL(9,6), location_lon DECIMAL(9,6), district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT &apos;Kerala&apos;, subscription_tier VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT &apos;free&apos;, subscription_expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ, trial_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), is_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT true, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), last_active TIMESTAMPTZ ); CREATE TABLE otp_sessions ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), phone VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, otp_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, -- bcrypt hashed expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL, used BOOLEAN DEFAULT false, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE refresh_tokens ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, token_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL, revoked BOOLEAN DEFAULT false, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Farm &amp; Field Management CREATE TABLE farms ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, location GEOMETRY(POINT, 4326), -- PostGIS point district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100), area_ha DECIMAL(8,2), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE fields ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), farm_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES farms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, name VARCHAR(100), boundaries GEOMETRY(POLYGON, 4326), -- Field boundary area_ha DECIMAL(8,2), slope VARCHAR(20), -- flat/gentle/moderate/steep irrigation VARCHAR(30), -- rainfed/canal/drip/sprinkler/tube-well soil_type VARCHAR(50), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE soil_profiles ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), field_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, pH DECIMAL(4,2), nitrogen_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), phosphorus_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), potassium_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), organic_carbon DECIMAL(5,2), texture VARCHAR(30), -- sandy/loamy/clayey/silty field_capacity DECIMAL(5,2), wilting_point DECIMAL(5,2), source VARCHAR(50), -- soil_health_card/lab_test/estimated measured_at DATE, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Crop Cycles &amp; Observations CREATE TABLE crop_cycles ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), field_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, crop VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, -- rice/banana/coconut/tomato/maize cultivar VARCHAR(100), season VARCHAR(20), -- kharif/rabi/zaid/perennial planting_date DATE NOT NULL, expected_harvest_date DATE, actual_harvest_date DATE, status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT &apos;active&apos;, -- active/harvested/abandoned notes TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE observations ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop_cycle_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crop_cycles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, location GEOMETRY(POINT, 4326), problem_description TEXT, voice_note_key VARCHAR(500), -- R2 object key irrigation_status VARCHAR(20), -- irrigated/dry/waterlogged fertilizer_recent BOOLEAN, pesticide_recent BOOLEAN, observed_growth_stage VARCHAR(50), observed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE images ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, r2_key VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL, mime_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, file_size_bytes INT, width_px INT, height_px INT, quality_score DECIMAL(4,2), -- 0.0-1.0 from quality checker blur_score DECIMAL(8,2), -- Laplacian variance created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Diagnoses &amp; Advisories CREATE TABLE diagnoses ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id), model_version VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, confidence_score DECIMAL(4,3) NOT NULL, -- 0.000-1.000 calibrated confidence_state VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, severity VARCHAR(20), -- low/medium/high/critical affected_area_pct DECIMAL(5,2), top_predictions JSONB, -- [{label, score}, ...] top-5 created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE weather_snapshots ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID REFERENCES observations(id), lat DECIMAL(9,6), lon DECIMAL(9,6), temperature_c DECIMAL(5,2), humidity_pct DECIMAL(5,2), rainfall_24h_mm DECIMAL(8,2), wind_speed_kmh DECIMAL(6,2), dew_point_c DECIMAL(5,2), solar_rad DECIMAL(8,2), consecutive_wet_days INT, forecast_json JSONB, -- 7-day forecast indicators_json JSONB, -- DFI, spray_window, heat_stress etc. provider VARCHAR(50), -- open_meteo/imd/cached snapshot_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE advisories ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id), confidence_state VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, recommended_scenario VARCHAR(5), -- A/B/C scenarios_json JSONB NOT NULL, evidence_bundle_json JSONB NOT NULL, recommended_action_json JSONB NOT NULL, what_would_change JSONB, -- list of strings safe_to_act BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true, sources_json JSONB, -- [{title, authority, url, tier}] model_version VARCHAR(50), knowledge_version VARCHAR(50), generation_ms INT, -- latency tracking created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Interventions &amp; Outcomes CREATE TABLE interventions ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop_cycle_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crop_cycles(id), advisory_id UUID REFERENCES advisories(id), action_type VARCHAR(50), -- spray/irrigate/fertilize/harvest/other description TEXT, product_used VARCHAR(200), quantity DECIMAL(10,3), quantity_unit VARCHAR(20), cost_inr DECIMAL(10,2), performed_at DATE NOT NULL, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE outcomes ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), intervention_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES interventions(id), observed_at DATE, improved VARCHAR(10), -- yes/no/partial/unsure notes TEXT, follow_up_image_id UUID REFERENCES images(id), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Simulation CREATE TABLE simulation_runs ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), advisory_id UUID REFERENCES advisories(id), simulator VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL, -- dssat/apsim/rules crop VARCHAR(50), input_json JSONB NOT NULL, output_json JSONB, error_message TEXT, runtime_ms INT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Knowledge Base CREATE TABLE knowledge_documents ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), title VARCHAR(500), crop VARCHAR(50), state VARCHAR(100), district VARCHAR(100), topic VARCHAR(100), -- disease/pest/fertilizer/irrigation/scheme growth_stage VARCHAR(50), source_tier INT NOT NULL, -- 1-6 source_authority VARCHAR(200), source_url VARCHAR(1000), language VARCHAR(10) DEFAULT &apos;en&apos;, published_at DATE, valid_until DATE, -- REQUIRED ΓÇö triggers stale check status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT &apos;PUBLISHED&apos;, content_hash VARCHAR(64), -- SHA-256 version INT DEFAULT 1, license VARCHAR(100), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE knowledge_chunks ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), document_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES knowledge_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, chunk_index INT NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL, embedding vector(1024), -- multilingual-e5-large dimension metadata_json JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); -- IVFFlat index for approximate nearest neighbor CREATE INDEX ON knowledge_chunks USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops) WITH (lists = 100); Schemes &amp; Market CREATE TABLE schemes ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), name VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL, short_name VARCHAR(50), authority VARCHAR(200), authority_level VARCHAR(20), -- central/state/district state VARCHAR(100), -- null = all India crops_applicable JSONB, -- [&quot;rice&quot;, &quot;maize&quot;] eligibility_rules JSONB, -- structured rules engine benefits_json JSONB, enrollment_url VARCHAR(500), helpline VARCHAR(50), valid_from DATE, valid_until DATE NOT NULL, -- mandatory freshness status VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT &apos;active&apos;, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE market_prices ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, market_name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100), price_date DATE NOT NULL, min_price DECIMAL(10,2), modal_price DECIMAL(10,2), max_price DECIMAL(10,2), unit VARCHAR(20) DEFAULT &apos;quintal&apos;, source VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT &apos;enam&apos;, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Notifications &amp; Audit CREATE TABLE notifications ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, farm_id UUID REFERENCES farms(id), type VARCHAR(50), -- morning_briefing/risk_alert/spray_window/scheme severity VARCHAR(20), -- info/watch/attention/action/urgent title VARCHAR(300), body TEXT, data_json JSONB, -- deep link payload fcm_message_id VARCHAR(200), delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ, opened_at TIMESTAMPTZ, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE audit_logs ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id), entity_type VARCHAR(50), entity_id UUID, action VARCHAR(50), -- create/read/update/delete/advisory/export ip_address INET, user_agent TEXT, details_json JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Expert Reviews CREATE TABLE expert_reviews ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), advisory_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES advisories(id), expert_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), verdict VARCHAR(20), -- agree/disagree/partial safety_ok BOOLEAN, notes TEXT, corrections JSONB, reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT NOW() ); Row-Level Security -- Enable RLS on all user-facing tables ALTER TABLE farms ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE fields ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE crop_cycles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE observations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE images ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE advisories ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; -- Users see only their own farms CREATE POLICY user_farms ON farms USING (user_id = current_setting(&apos;app.current_user_id&apos;)::UUID); -- Cascade via farm ownership for fields, cycles, observations CREATE POLICY user_fields ON fields USING (farm_id IN (SELECT id FROM farms WHERE user_id = current_setting(&apos;app.current_user_id&apos;)::UUID)); 4. API SPECIFICATION 4.1 Authentication Flow Step Endpoint Request Response Notes 1. Send OTP POST /api/v1/auth/send-otp { phone: &apos;+919876543210&apos; } { message: &apos;OTP sent&apos;, expires_in: 300 } 6-digit OTP, 5min expiry, SMS via MSG91/free tier 2. Verify OTP POST /api/v1/auth/verify-otp { phone, otp } { access_token, refresh_token, user } JWT 15min access + 7day refresh 3. Refresh POST /api/v1/auth/refresh { refresh_token } { access_token } Refresh token rotates on use 4. Logout POST /api/v1/auth/logout { refresh_token } { message } Revokes refresh token 4.2 Core Endpoint Specifications Method Path Auth Request Body Response Status Codes POST /api/v1/farms JWT { name, location_lat, location_lon, area_ha } Farm object 201 / 400 / 401 POST /api/v1/farms/{id}/fields JWT { name, area_ha, slope, irrigation, soil_type } Field object 201 / 400 / 404 POST /api/v1/crop-cycles JWT { field_id, crop, cultivar, season, planting_date } CropCycle object 201 / 400 POST /api/v1/observations JWT + multipart { crop_cycle_id, description, images[] } Observation + image_ids 201 / 400 / 413 POST /api/v1/diagnose JWT { observation_id } AdvisoryResponse (full schema) 200 / 400 / 422 / 503 GET /api/v1/weather/{lat}/{lon} JWT ΓÇö WeatherSnapshot + Indicators 200 / 503 GET /api/v1/risk/{farm_id} JWT ΓÇö RiskProfile 200 / 404 GET /api/v1/schemes JWT ?state=&amp;crop=&amp;problem= SchemeMatch[] 200 GET /api/v1/markets/{crop} JWT ?state=&amp;district= MarketObservation[] 200 POST /api/v1/simulate JWT SimulationParams JSON { scenarios: [] } 200 / 400 / 503 GET /api/v1/farms/{id}/timeline JWT ?from=&amp;to= TimelineEvent[] 200 / 404 GET /api/v1/farms/{id}/season-summary JWT ?cycle_id= SeasonSummary 200 POST /api/v1/interventions JWT { crop_cycle_id, advisory_id, action_type, ... } Intervention object 201 POST /api/v1/outcomes JWT { intervention_id, improved, notes } Outcome object 201 GET /api/v1/sync/delta JWT ?last_sync=ISO8601 { updates, deletes } 200 GET /api/v1/notifications JWT ?unread=true Notification[] 200 4.3 POST &quot;expected_cost_inr&quot;: 0, &quot;expected_net_return_inr&quot;: 64000, &quot;risk_level&quot;: &quot;high&quot; }, { &quot;id&quot;: &quot;B&quot;, &quot;label&quot;: &quot;Treat now knapsack sprayer, cover underside of leaves&quot;, &quot;expected_yield_kgha&quot;: 5100, // MODELLED ESTIMATE &quot;expected_cost_inr&quot;: 480, &quot;expected_net_return_inr&quot;: 101520, AGENT TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION 5.1 LangGraph State Schema class AgriSAGEState(TypedDict): # Input farm_state: FarmStateSchema images: List[ImageData] # Agent outputs (populated progressively) vision_result: Optional[VisionResult] weather_result: Optional[WeatherResult] crop_stage_result: Optional[CropStageResult] knowledge_result: Optional[KnowledgeResult] critic_result: Optional[CriticResult] scheme_result: Optional[SchemeResult] simulation_result: Optional[SimulationResult] decision_result: Optional[DecisionResult] # Final output advisory: Optional[AdvisoryResponse] # Control errors: List[str] warnings: List[str] run_simulation: bool # set by Critic based on decision weight 5.2 Agent I/O Contracts ΓÇö All Pydantic Agent Input Schema Output Schema LLM Called? Intake Raw request JSON FarmStateSchema (typed, validated) NO ΓÇö pure parsing Vision FarmStateSchema + images VisionResult {label, confidence, severity, top_k} NO ΓÇö ONNX model Weather FarmStateSchema WeatherResult {snapshot, indicators, forecast} NO ΓÇö API + rules Crop Stage FarmStateSchema + weather CropStageResult {stage, gdd, vulnerability, windows} NO ΓÇö GDD formula Knowledge FarmStateSchema + vision + stage KnowledgeResult {chunks, sources, authority_tiers} NO ΓÇö retrieval only Critic All upstream results CriticResult {confidence_state, conflicts, missing, safe_to_act} NO ΓÇö rule-based Scheme FarmStateSchema + critic SchemeResult {matches, eligibility_notes} YES ΓÇö complex eligibility Decision All results + simulation DecisionResult {scenarios, recommended, ranking_score} YES ΓÇö synthesis Explanation DecisionResult + language ExplanationResult {advisory_text, voice_text, local_translations} YES ΓÇö generation Simulation FarmStateSchema + interventions SimulationResult {scenarios_a_b_c, yield_est, stress} NO ΓÇö DSSAT subprocess Market FarmStateSchema MarketResult {current_price, trend, best_market} NO ΓÇö data lookup Economics SimulationResult + market EconomicsResult {net_return_per_scenario, cost_breakdown} NO ΓÇö arithmetic Monitoring FarmStateSchema + weather + stage MonitoringResult {risk_scores, alerts, briefing} NO ΓÇö rule-based 5.3 Critic Agent Logic ΓÇö Safety Rules class CriticAgent: CONFLICTS = [ # Vision says fungal but weather is unfavorable Rule(vision=&apos;fungal&apos;, weather_dfi=&apos;&lt;0.3&apos;, action=&apos;REDUCE_CONFIDENCE&apos;), # Disease uncommon at current stage Rule(vision_label_not_in=stage_disease_map[stage], action=&apos;FLAG_STAGE_MISMATCH&apos;), ] SAFETY_CHECKS = [ check_chemical_in_cibrc_database, # BLOCKS if not found check_dosage_within_label_range, # BLOCKS if exceeded check_crop_chemical_registration, # BLOCKS if not registered check_preharvest_interval, # WARNS if PHI not met check_reentry_interval, # WARNS if REI not met check_scheme_exists_in_database, # WARNS if not found check_document_not_expired, # WARNS if valid_until passed check_no_llm_invented_products, # BLOCKS ΓÇö name must resolve to DB entry ] def run(self, state: AgriSAGEState) -&gt; CriticResult: conflicts = self._detect_conflicts(state) safe = all(check(state) for check in self.SAFETY_CHECKS) confidence = self._calibrate(state, conflicts) missing = self._find_missing_data(state) run_sim = confidence.score &gt; 0.65 and not conflicts return CriticResult(confidence=confidence, conflicts=conflicts, safe_to_act=safe, missing=missing, run_simulation=run_sim) 6. ML/AI TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION 6.1 Vision Model Training # Training script: backend/ml/vision/train.py MODEL = &apos;efficientnet_b3&apos; IMAGE_SIZE = 300 # EfficientNet-B3 native resolution BATCH_SIZE = 32 LEARNING_RATE = 1e-4 EPOCHS = 50 EARLY_STOPPING_PATIENCE = 7 # Data splits ΓÇö FARMER-LEVEL, not random # farmers_train / farmers_val / farmers_test ΓÇö no overlap # This prevents inflated accuracy from near-duplicate images AUGMENTATION = { &apos;train&apos;: [RandomCrop(300), RandomHorizontalFlip(), ColorJitter(0.3), GaussianBlur(p=0.2), RandomRotation(30)], &apos;val&apos;: [CenterCrop(300)], # No augmentation for evaluation } # Post-training calibration (REQUIRED) # Temperature scaling on validation set # Target ECE &lt; 0.10 before deployment from temperature_scaling import ModelWithTemperature calibrated_model = ModelWithTemperature(model) calibrated_model.set_temperature(val_loader) 6.2 ONNX Export Pipeline # backend/ml/vision/export.py def export_to_onnx(model_path: str, output_path: str): model = load_calibrated_model(model_path) model.eval() dummy = torch.randn(1, 3, 300, 300) torch.onnx.export( model, dummy, output_path, opset_version=17, input_names=[&apos;image&apos;], output_names=[&apos;logits&apos;], dynamic_axes={&apos;image&apos;: {0: &apos;batch&apos;}} ) def quantize_int8(onnx_path: str, quantized_path: str): from onnxruntime.quantization import quantize_dynamic, QuantType quantize_dynamic(onnx_path, quantized_path, weight_type=QuantType.QInt8) # Verify accuracy loss &lt; 2% after quantization verify_quantized_accuracy(onnx_path, quantized_path, test_loader) 6.3 RAG Retrieval Pipeline # backend/knowledge/retrieval.py class HybridRetriever: def __init__(self): self.dense = MultilingualE5Large() # vector search via pgvector self.sparse = BM25Index() # keyword search self.reranker = CrossEncoder(&apos;cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2&apos;) async def retrieve(self, query: FarmStateQuery, top_k: int = 5): # Parallel dense + sparse retrieval dense_hits = await self.dense.search(query.to_text(), k=top_k*3, filters={&apos;crop&apos;: query.crop, &apos;state&apos;: query.state, &apos;status&apos;: &apos;PUBLISHED&apos;, &apos;valid_until__gt&apos;: date.today()}) sparse_hits = self.sparse.search(query.to_keywords(), k=top_k*3) # Merge by reciprocal rank fusion merged = reciprocal_rank_fusion([dense_hits, sparse_hits]) # Cross-encoder reranking reranked = self.reranker.rerank(query.to_text(), merged) # Source authority boost: Tier 1 documents score * 1.3 boosted = apply_authority_boost(reranked) return boosted[:top_k] 6.4 LLM System Prompt Architecture # Advisory synthesis prompt structure (backend/agents/explanation.py) SYSTEM_PROMPT = &quot;&quot;&quot; You are Agri-SAGE, an agricultural advisory system. Your role: Convert the structured evidence below into a clear farmer advisory. Language: {language} Farmer profile: {farmer_context} STRICT RULES: 1. Only use information from the provided evidence bundle. Do not add facts. 2. Only mention chemical products that appear in the provided CIB&amp;RC data. 3. If confidence_state is UNCERTAIN or INSUFFICIENT_DATA, do not recommend treatment. 4. Label all yield estimates as &apos;modelled estimate&apos;. 5. Always end with: what_would_change_this based on the provided missing_data list. 6. Output ONLY valid JSON matching the AdvisoryTextSchema. No markdown. &quot;&quot;&quot; # The LLM receives structured input ΓÇö never raw user text USER_PROMPT = &quot;&quot;&quot; Evidence bundle: {evidence_json} Decision result: {decision_json} Sources: {sources_json} Generate advisory in {language}. Output JSON only. &quot;&quot;&quot; 7. MOBILE TECHNICAL SPECIFICATION 7.1 Android App Architecture Layer Pattern Implementation UI MVVM + Unidirectional Data Flow Jetpack Compose + StateFlow + ViewModel Domain Use Cases AgriSAGEUseCases: DiagnoseUseCase, GetFarmStateUseCase, SyncUseCase Data (Remote) Repository pattern FarmRepository, AdvisoryRepository ΓÇö Retrofit + OkHttp Data (Local) Repository pattern LocalFarmRepository ΓÇö Room DAO Sync Offline queue + WorkManager SyncWorker: uploads queued actions on connectivity ML Inference ONNX Runtime session VisionInferenceManager ΓÇö reuses session across calls Voice Provider abstraction SpeechManager: BhashiniProvider / AndroidSpeechProvider Navigation Navigation Compose NavGraph with typed arguments DI Hilt Module-per-feature, ViewModelFactory injection 7.2 Android Permissions Required Permission Reason Optional? CAMERA Crop photo capture NO ΓÇö core feature RECORD_AUDIO Voice input for queries YES ΓÇö voice is optional ACCESS_FINE_LOCATION GPS for weather and scheme matching YES ΓÇö can enter manually ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE Connectivity detection for online/offline mode NO RECEIVE_BOOT_COMPLETED Morning briefing scheduling on device restart YES POST_NOTIFICATIONS Push alerts ΓÇö Android 13+ requires explicit permission YES FOREGROUND_SERVICE Background sync on low-end devices YES 7.3 Room Database Schema (Android) @Entity(tableName = &quot;farmers&quot;) data class FarmerEntity( @PrimaryKey val id: String, val name: String?, val phone: String, val language: String, val subscriptionTier: String, val updatedAt: Long ) @Entity(tableName = &quot;farms&quot;) data class FarmEntity( @PrimaryKey val id: String, val userId: String, val name: String, val locationLat: Double?, val locationLon: Double?, val areaHa: Double?, val isSynced: Boolean = false, val createdAt: Long ) @Entity(tableName = &quot;sync_queue&quot;) data class SyncQueueItem( @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Int = 0, val entityType: String, val entityId: String, val action: String, // create/update/delete val payloadJson: String, val priority: Int = 5, // 1=highest (observations), 10=lowest val retryCount: Int = 0, val createdAt: Long ) 7.4 ONNX Inference Implementation class VisionInferenceManager(context: Context) { private val session: OrtSession private val env: OrtEnvironment = OrtEnvironment.getEnvironment() init { val options = OrtSession.SessionOptions().apply { addNnapi() // Hardware acceleration where available setInterOpNumThreads(2) } val modelBytes = context.assets.open(&quot;vision_int8.onnx&quot;).readBytes() session = env.createSession(modelBytes, options) } fun classify(bitmap: Bitmap): VisionResult { val tensor = preprocessImage(bitmap) // normalize to [0,1], resize 300x300 val output = session.run(mapOf(&quot;image&quot; to tensor)) val logits = output[0].value as Array&lt;FloatArray&gt; return parseResult(logits[0]) } // Singleton ΓÇö reuse session for performance } 8. IMD: apply at imdaws.com R2_BUCKET YES Cloudflare R2 bucket name Cloudflare dashboard ΓåÆ R2 ΓåÆ Create bucket R2_ACCESS_KEY YES R2 API token Access Key ID Cloudflare dashboard ΓåÆ R2 ΓåÆ Manage API Tokens R2_SECRET_KEY YES R2 API token Secret Same as above R2_ACCOUNT_ID YES Cloudflare account ID Cloudflare dashboard ΓåÆ right sidebar FCM_KEY Phase 1 Firebase Server Key for push notifications Firebase console ΓåÆ Project Settings ΓåÆ Cloud Messaging BHASHINI_API_KEY Phase 3 Bhashini API key for STT/TTS Apply: bhashini.gov.in ΓÇö allow 4-6 weeks OTP_PROVIDER_KEY YES SMS OTP provider API key MSG91: msg91.com (cheapest India SMS) ENVIRONMENT YES development / staging / production Set in deployment config LOG_LEVEL NO DEBUG / INFO / WARNING Default: INFO in production MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB NO Max upload size in MB Default: 5 ΓÜá Never commit .env file to git. Add .env to .gitignore from day one. Use .env.example with placeholder values for documentation. 9. TESTING SPECIFICATION 9.1 Test Categories &amp; Coverage Targets Category Coverage Target Tools Key Test Cases Unit ΓÇö Business Logic 80% line coverage pytest GDD calculation, DFI formula, confidence calibration, cost scoring Unit ΓÇö Pydantic Schemas 100% schema validation pytest All advisory fields present; invalid input rejected with 422 Integration ΓÇö API All endpoints pytest + httpx Happy path + error cases + auth failures Integration ΓÇö Agent Each agent isolated pytest + mocks Each agent with known input ΓåÆ expected output type Vision Model Golden image set pytest + ONNX RT 100 disease images, 50 healthy, 30 OOD ΓÇö known labels RAG Retrieval Retrieval benchmark set pytest 50 queries ΓåÆ known relevant chunks ΓåÆ recall@5 &gt; 0.90 Safety ΓÇö Chemical CIB&amp;RC boundary tests pytest Unknown product ΓåÆ BLOCKED; valid product ΓåÆ PASSES Safety ΓÇö Hallucination Fabricated claim injection pytest LLM output with invented product ΓåÆ Critic blocks Offline Mode Connectivity-off tests Espresso (Android) All core features work with airplane mode on Performance ΓÇö Vision Device benchmarks Android profiler &lt; 4s on Snapdragon 665, &lt; 1.5s on Snapdragon 778G Performance ΓÇö API Load test locust 100 concurrent advisory requests ΓåÆ P95 &lt; 8s Security ΓÇö Auth JWT edge cases pytest Expired token, tampered token, missing token ΓåÆ 401 Security ΓÇö RLS Cross-user data access pytest User A cannot read User B&apos;s farm data Regression Full test suite pytest + GitHub Actions Run on every PR ΓÇö merge blocked if any test fails 9.2 Golden Test Set Requirements Minimum 100 disease-positive images per supported crop with expert-verified labels Minimum 50 healthy crop images per crop Minimum 30 OOD images (human hands, walls, food, animals) Minimum 20 poor-quality images (blurry, dark, partial) All images from FIELD conditions ΓÇö not studio/laboratory Farmer-level split maintained in golden set ΓÇö no leakage Golden set frozen after creation ΓÇö only additions allowed, no modifications ΓÜá The golden test set is sacred. Every model deployment must pass against it. Store in /tests/golden/ with SHA-256 manifest. Any change to the set requires documentation of reason. 10. SECURITY TECHNICAL REQUIREMENTS Requirement Implementation Test JWT validation HS256 with 32-byte secret; exp claim checked; no algorithm confusion attack pytest: expired, wrong alg, missing claims ΓåÆ 401 OTP brute force prevention Max 5 attempts per phone per 10 minutes; HMAC-SHA256 hashed; 5-minute expiry pytest: 6th attempt ΓåÆ 429 SQL injection SQLAlchemy parameterised queries everywhere ΓÇö no string f-formatting of SQL pytest: injection strings in all input fields ΓåÆ 400, not 500 File upload security MIME type validation (not just extension); max 5MB; metadata strip with ExifTool; virus scan via ClamAV pytest: PHP file ΓåÆ rejected; &gt;5MB ΓåÆ 413 Image URL access Signed Cloudflare R2 URLs with 1-hour expiry ΓÇö no public bucket pytest: unsigned URL ΓåÆ 403; expired URL ΓåÆ 403 Rate limiting nginx: 10 req/s per IP general; 2 req/s for /diagnose; 5/10min for OTP locust: exceed rate ΓåÆ 429 CORS Explicit allow-list ΓÇö no wildcard in production pytest: unknown origin ΓåÆ 403 Dependency scanning pip-audit in CI/CD on every PR GitHub Actions: critical CVE ΓåÆ fail build Secret management All secrets in environment variables; no hardcoded credentials anywhere grep scan in CI: any plaintext credential pattern ΓåÆ fail build Prompt injection User text never inserted as LLM instruction; retrieved docs never executed as code pytest: injection string in problem_description ΓåÆ parsed as data, not instruction AGRI-SAGE TRD v1.0 ΓÇö IMPLEMENTATION READY


=== 3_AgriSAGE_AppFlow.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE COMPLETE APP FLOW DOCUMENT Every Screen ┬╖ Every State ┬╖ Every Decision Path Field Detail Document App Flow ΓÇö User Journey &amp; Screen Architecture Audience UI/UX Designer, Android Developer, VSCode AI Agent Platform Android (Primary) ΓÇö Web Dashboard (Secondary, Phase 3) Version 1.0 ΓÇö MVP + V2 Complete FLOW READING GUIDE This document maps every screen, state, and decision path in the Agri-SAGE Android application. Read flows as: SCREEN NAME ΓåÆ [User Action] ΓåÆ NEXT SCREEN or [System Response]. Screen names are in CAPS and bold User actions are in [Square Brackets] System responses are in {Curly Braces} Decision points are in &lt;Angle Brackets&gt; with YES/NO branches Error states are marked ΓÜá Box Color Meaning Color Code Description SCREEN e8f5e8 (light green) A full screen the farmer sees ACTION d4edda (tip green) Something the farmer taps or does SYSTEM e8f0fe (light blue) Something the system does automatically ERROR fff3cd (yellow) An error or edge case DECISION f0f0f0 (grey) A branching decision point 1. YES ΓåÆ&gt; [SYSTEM] System: Create user account. Generate JWT access + refresh tokens. Store refresh token securely. Generate first morning briefing. [SCREEN] SETUP COMPLETE: Checkmark animation. &apos;Your farm is ready!&apos; Brief summary of what was set up. &apos;Go to My Farm&apos; button. ΓåÆ Navigate to HOME SCREEN 1.2 Returning User ΓÇö Active Session [SCREEN] SPLASH SCREEN (2 seconds) [SYSTEM] System: Validate JWT token. If expired, refresh silently. Load cached farm state. ΓåÆ Navigate directly to HOME SCREEN 1.3 Returning User ΓÇö Expired Session &lt;JWT refresh fails? YES ΓåÆ&gt; [SCREEN] PHONE REGISTRATION (with message &apos;Please verify your number again&apos;) ΓåÆ Same OTP flow as new user. Farm data preserved. 2. HOME SCREEN FLOW 2.1 Home Screen States [SCREEN] HOME SCREEN ΓÇö ONLINE STATE: Farm name + crop + stage header. Morning briefing card (if generated today). Top risk card. DIAGNOSE FLOW ΓÇö CORE FEATURE 3.1 Full Diagnosis Journey [SCREEN] DIAGNOSE START: Two options visible. &apos;≡ƒô╖ Take a Photo&apos; (large, primary). &apos;≡ƒÄñ Describe by Voice&apos; (secondary). &apos;How this works&apos; info link. [Farmer positions camera ΓÇö indicator turns GREEN] [Farmer taps capture button] [SYSTEM] System: Capture image. Run quality check one final time. ΓåÆ&gt; [ERROR] ΓÜá QUALITY REJECTION: Full-screen overlay with specific issue: &apos;This photo is too blurry. Please move closer and hold the phone steady.&apos; Large retry button. [Farmer confirms details, taps &apos;Analyze My Crop&apos;] [SYSTEM] System: &lt;Online? YES ΓåÆ Upload image ΓåÆ Start server-side advisory pipeline&gt;. &lt;Offline? ΓåÆ Run local ONNX vision ΓåÆ Use cached knowledge ΓåÆ Generate offline advisory&gt;. Show progress screen. [SCREEN] ANALYZING SCREEN: Crop illustration animation.
