ΓåÆ Advisory generation complete (target: &lt;8 seconds online, &lt;3 seconds offline) [SCREEN] ADVISORY RESULT SCREEN (full detail in Section 3.2) 3.2 Advisory Result Screen ΓÇö Detailed Layout [SCREEN] ADVISORY SCREEN ΓÇö TOP SECTION: Confidence badge (LIKELY / POSSIBLE / etc.) in colored pill. Was this helpful? ≡ƒæì ≡ƒæÄ quick feedback. 3.3 Advisory States ΓÇö Special Cases [ERROR] ΓÜá INSUFFICIENT_DATA STATE: &apos;We need more information.&apos; Clear explanation of what&apos;s missing. Checks: risk level, weather, crop stage, market changes. Sends FCM notification. [SCREEN] NOTIFICATION (on phone lock screen): &apos;Good morning, Rajan ≡ƒî╛ Your paddy is at tillering stage. Blast risk: Medium. Check lower leaves today.&apos; [Farmer taps notification] ΓåÆ Opens directly to MORNING BRIEFING DETAIL SCREEN (not home screen ΓÇö deeplink) [SCREEN] MORNING BRIEFING DETAIL: Full briefing card. Voice playback button at top. Crop summary. Risk analysis. Weather for the day. One recommended action. Market update if significant. &apos;Start my check&apos; button ΓåÆ DIAGNOSE FLOW. 9.2 Risk Alert Notification [SYSTEM] System: Monitoring Agent detects DFI &gt; 0.7 AND crop at susceptible stage. Generates alert. [SCREEN] NOTIFICATION: &apos;ΓÜá Risk Alert ΓÇö Your rice crop. Blast conditions are favorable. Inspect today.&apos; Severity color: ORANGE for ATTENTION, RED for URGENT. [Farmer taps notification] ΓåÆ Opens RISK ALERT DETAIL with full explanation and recommended action. One-tap to DIAGNOSE FLOW. 9.3 Notification Settings &amp; Anti-Spam Maximum 1 morning briefing per day per farm Maximum 2 risk alerts per day per farm (highest priority only) Spray window alerts only when there is an active diagnosis pending treatment Market alerts only when price change &gt; 10% from 7-day average Quiet hours respected ΓÇö no notifications between 9pm and 6am Farmer can disable any notification type individually 10. VOICE FLOW [SCREEN] VOICE INPUT ENTRY: Microphone button on diagnose start screen or keyboard + mic option on any text field. Malayalam language prompt. &apos;Tap and speak&apos; instruction. [Farmer taps mic, says &apos;α┤Äα┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡å α┤¿α╡åα┤▓α╡ìα┤▓α┤┐α╡╜ α┤çα┤Öα╡ìα┤Öα┤¿α╡å α┤¬α┤╛α┤ƒα╡üα┤òα╡╛ α┤╡α┤¿α╡ìα┤¿α┤┐α┤ƒα╡ìα┤ƒα╡üα┤úα╡ìα┤ƒα╡ì&apos;] [SYSTEM] System: &lt;Online? ΓåÆ Bhashini STT API call&gt;. &lt;Offline? ΓåÆ Android SpeechRecognizer&gt;. Transcribe audio. Extract structured intent (crop, problem, location) ΓÇö DO NOT pass raw transcript to LLM. [SCREEN] TRANSCRIPT REVIEW: Shows transcription in Malayalam. Edit option if wrong. Detected intent shown: &apos;Crop: Rice | Problem: Spots on leaves&apos;. &apos;Yes, that&apos;s right&apos; / &apos;Let me correct this&apos; options. [Farmer confirms] ΓåÆ CONTEXT FORM pre-filled ΓåÆ DIAGNOSE FLOW continues [SCREEN] VOICE OUTPUT: Speaker icon on every advisory screen. Plays TTS in farmer&apos;s selected language. Pause/resume. [SCREEN] PAYMENT SUCCESS: Checkmark animation. &apos;You now have unlimited advisories!&apos; &apos;Start using Agri-SAGE Pro&apos; button. [ERROR] ΓÜá FREE LIMIT REACHED: Farmer tries 6th advisory on free plan. &apos;You&apos;ve used your 5 free advisories this month.&apos; Subscription options shown. &apos;Continue trial (14 days free)&apos; prominent option. 12. EXPERT ESCALATION FLOW [SCREEN] ESCALATION ENTRY: Shown when Critic returns CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE or farmer taps &apos;Get expert help&apos;. &apos;Send this to an agronomist for review?&apos; Brief explanation of process (1-2 business days). Farmer&apos;s advisory already attached. [Farmer taps &apos;Send to expert&apos;] [SYSTEM] System: Create ExpertReview record. Add to expert review queue. Notify available agronomist (push notification to agronomist app). [SCREEN] PENDING STATE: &apos;Your query has been sent to an agronomist. You&apos;ll be notified when they respond ΓÇö usually within 1 business day.&apos; Return home option. [SYSTEM] System: Agronomist reviews on Expert Dashboard (web). Submits verdict and notes. [SCREEN] EXPERT RESPONSE NOTIFICATION: &apos;An agronomist has reviewed your query.&apos; Opens updated advisory with expert notes and verification badge. 13. OFFLINE SYNC FLOW [SYSTEM] System: All farmer actions while offline stored in sync_queue table (Room DB). Priority: 1=Observations, 3=Interventions, 5=Outcomes, 8=Feedback. [SYSTEM] System: WorkManager SyncWorker monitors connectivity (ConnectivityManager). On network available ΓåÆ start sync. [SCREEN] SYNC INDICATOR: Small sync icon in top bar animates while syncing. Disappears when done. Step 1: Upload queued observations and images (highest priority) Step 2: Upload intervention and outcome records Step 3: Upload feedback and ratings Step 4: Download updated weather, risk scores Step 5: Download updated knowledge cache if version changed Step 6: Download new scheme data if available [ERROR] ΓÜá SYNC CONFLICT: If same farm data changed on server and locally ΓåÆ server wins for profile data. Local observations are always preserved (append-only). User notified only if significant conflict. [ERROR] ΓÜá SYNC FAILURE: Exponential backoff ΓÇö retry at 1min, 2min, 5min, 15min, 1hr. After 24 hours without sync, banner shown: &apos;Your data has not synced. Connect to internet when possible.&apos; 14. ERROR STATES REFERENCE Error Code User-Facing Message (Malayalam + English) Recovery Action ERR-001: Server unreachable &apos;α┤çα┤¬α╡ìα┤¬α╡ïα╡╛ α┤çα┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡╝α┤¿α╡åα┤▒α╡ìα┤▒α╡ì α┤▓α┤¡α╡ìα┤»α┤«α┤▓α╡ìα┤▓ / No internet&apos; ΓÇö showing cached data Auto-switch to offline mode. Banner shown. ERR-002: Advisory timeout &apos;Analysis is taking longer than usual. Please try again.&apos; Retry button. If 3rd failure ΓåÆ queue for background processing ERR-003: Image upload fail &apos;Photo upload failed. Saved locally ΓÇö will upload when connected.&apos; Queue image for sync. Advisory continues with local inference. ERR-004: Vision model fail &apos;Could not analyze this photo. Please try with a different angle.&apos; Retry suggestion. If persistent ΓåÆ fallback to text-based advisory ERR-005: OTP expired &apos;This code has expired. Please request a new one.&apos; Resend button shown. Previous attempt cleared. ERR-006: Subscription limit &apos;You&apos;ve used your free advisories this month.&apos; Subscription upgrade shown. 14-day trial offered. ERR-007: Unsupported crop &apos;We don&apos;t support this crop yet. Coming soon!&apos; Supported crops list. &apos;Request this crop&apos; feedback option. ERR-008: Scheme data stale &apos;This scheme information may be outdated. Please verify officially.&apos; Official portal link. Local agriculture office contact. ERR-009: Auth failure &apos;Please verify your phone number again.&apos; Re-OTP flow. Farm data preserved. ERR-010: Storage full &apos;Your device storage is low. Please free up space.&apos; Guide to clear cache in settings. AGRI-SAGE APP FLOW v1.0 ΓÇö COMPLETE Every screen. Every state. Every path.


=== 4_AgriSAGE_UIUX_Spec.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE UI/UX SPECIFICATION Design System ┬╖ Component Library ┬╖ All Screen Layouts ┬╖ Interaction Patterns ┬╖ Accessibility ┬╖ Localization Field Detail Document UI/UX Specification v1.0 Audience Android Developer, VSCode AI Agent Framework Jetpack Compose + Material Design 3 Minimum Device Android 8.0 API 26 ΓÇö 1GB RAM baseline Primary Language Malayalam (ml) ΓÇö all text must have Malayalam equivalent 1. DESIGN SYSTEM ΓÇö COLOR TOKENS 1.1 Complete Color Palette Token Hex Usage color_primary #2D7D2D Primary buttons, active nav, key headings color_primary_dark #1A5C1A App bar, header sections, strong emphasis color_primary_light #E8F5E8 Card backgrounds, list alternates, soft fills color_primary_container #C8E6C9 Chip backgrounds, selected states color_on_primary #FFFFFF Text/icons on primary-colored surfaces color_secondary #558B2F Secondary buttons, tags, secondary actions color_secondary_light #F1F8E9 Secondary card backgrounds color_risk_critical #C62828 CRITICAL risk, urgent alerts, error states color_risk_high #E65100 HIGH risk, action-required alerts color_risk_medium #F57F17 MEDIUM risk, watch alerts color_risk_low #2E7D32 LOW risk, positive states, success color_confidence_confirmed #1B5E20 CONFIRMED badge color_confidence_likely #2E7D32 LIKELY badge color_confidence_possible #F57F17 POSSIBLE badge color_confidence_uncertain #BF360C UNCERTAIN badge color_confidence_insufficient #757575 INSUFFICIENT_DATA badge color_surface #FAFAFA Screen backgrounds color_surface_variant #F5F5F5 Elevated surfaces, input backgrounds color_on_surface #212121 Primary text on light backgrounds color_on_surface_secondary #616161 Secondary text, captions, metadata color_on_surface_tertiary #9E9E9E Placeholder, disabled states color_outline #BDBDBD Input borders, dividers color_outline_variant #E0E0E0 Subtle dividers, table row lines color_warning_bg #FFF9C4 Warning card backgrounds color_warning_text #F57F17 Warning text and icons color_info_bg #E3F2FD Info card backgrounds color_offline_banner #FFF3E0 Offline mode banner background color_offline_text #E65100 Offline mode banner text color_cached_label #FF8F00 Cached data labels color_scheme_bg #EDE7F6 Government scheme card backgrounds color_scheme_text #4527A0 Government scheme text and icons 2. TYPOGRAPHY Style Name Font Weight Size (sp) Line Height (sp) Usage displayLarge Noto Sans Bold 32 40 Splash screen logo only headlineLarge Noto Sans Bold 24 32 Screen titles, major headings headlineMedium Noto Sans SemiBold 20 28 Section headings, card titles headlineSmall Noto Sans SemiBold 18 24 Sub-section headings bodyLarge Noto Sans Regular 16 24 PRIMARY body text ΓÇö all main reading bodyMedium Noto Sans Regular 14 20 Secondary body, descriptions bodySmall Noto Sans Regular 12 16 Captions, metadata, timestamps labelLarge Noto Sans Medium 14 20 Button labels, tab labels labelMedium Noto Sans Medium 12 16 Chip labels, badge text labelSmall Noto Sans Medium 10 14 Icon labels, micro text monoCode Noto Sans Mono Regular 13 20 Dosage values, coordinates, versions ΓÜá Always use Noto Sans ΓÇö never system default font. Noto Sans covers Malayalam, Tamil, Telugu, Hindi, and Latin scripts. System fonts vary across Android devices and may not support regional scripts. 3. SPACING &amp; LAYOUT Token Value Usage spacing_xs 4dp Micro gaps between tightly grouped elements spacing_sm 8dp Compact internal padding, icon-to-text gaps spacing_md 16dp Standard card internal padding, form field gaps spacing_lg 24dp Section separation, generous card padding spacing_xl 32dp Screen-level section gaps spacing_xxl 48dp Major separators, hero areas corner_sm 8dp Chips, small buttons, OTP boxes corner_md 12dp Cards, input fields, standard buttons corner_lg 16dp Large cards, bottom sheets, dialogs corner_xl 24dp FABs, large action buttons, bottom sheets screen_padding_h 16dp Left and right padding ΓÇö ALL screens card_elevation 2dp Standard card shadow card_elevation_active 4dp Selected or hovered card bottom_nav_height 56dp Fixed bottom navigation bar height top_app_bar_height 56dp Standard app bar height min_touch_target 48dp MINIMUM tappable size ΓÇö no exceptions ΓÇö WCAG AA 4. Scheme list 3 skeleton cards 80dp each &apos;No schemes matched your profile yet&apos; &apos;Could not load schemes ΓÇö tap to retry&apos; Farm timeline Skeleton timeline items &apos;No observations yet ΓÇö check your crop!&apos; + diagnose button Retry button Image upload Progress bar overlay on thumbnail N/A &apos;Upload failed ΓÇö saved locally, will sync&apos; Market prices Shimmer on price cards &apos;Market data unavailable today&apos; &apos;Could not fetch prices&apos; 7. ACCESSIBILITY REQUIREMENTS Minimum touch target: 48├ù48dp on ALL interactive elements ΓÇö no exceptions per WCAG 2.1 Color contrast: minimum 4.5:1 for body text, 3:1 for large text (24sp+) against background Color independence: color is NEVER the only indicator ΓÇö always paired with icon OR text shape TalkBack: every screen navigable with swipe/double-tap ΓÇö all images have contentDescription Focus order: logical reading order (topΓåÆbottom, leftΓåÆright) enforced in Compose semantics Font scaling: ALL text uses sp units ΓÇö app works correctly up to 1.5├ù system font scale Voice: every advisory screen has voice playback ΓÇö app is usable by users who cannot read Reduce motion: all animations respect system &apos;Reduce motion&apos; / &apos;Remove animations&apos; setting Language: every string has Malayalam translation ΓÇö no English fallback visible to Malayalam user Error messages: always specific and actionable ΓÇö never &apos;Error occurred&apos; alone ΓÜá Before any public release: conduct usability test with minimum 5 actual farmers with low digital literacy. Tests on developers&apos; own devices are insufficient. 8. LOCALIZATION Category Key Example English Malayalam Navigation nav_diagnose Check Crop α┤╡α┤┐α┤│ α┤¬α┤░α┤┐α┤╢α╡ïα┤ºα┤┐α┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤ò Advisory adv_confidence_likely Likely α┤╕α┤╛α┤ºα╡ìα┤»α┤ñ α┤òα╡éα┤ƒα╡üα┤ñα╡╜ Advisory adv_confidence_uncertain Uncertain α┤ëα┤▒α┤¬α╡ìα┤¬α┤┐α┤▓α╡ìα┤▓ Advisory adv_modelled Modelled estimate * α┤«α╡ïα┤íα╡╜ α┤òα┤úα┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡ìΓÇî * Advisory adv_what_to_do What to do α┤Äα┤¿α╡ìα┤ñα╡ì α┤Üα╡åα┤»α╡ìα┤»α┤úα┤é Advisory adv_when When α┤Äα┤¬α╡ìα┤¬α╡ïα╡╛ Quality cam_blur Photo is blurry ΓÇö hold steady α┤½α╡ïα┤ƒα╡ìα┤ƒα╡ï α┤«α┤Öα╡ìα┤Öα┤┐α┤»α┤┐α┤░α┤┐α┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤¿α╡ìα┤¿α╡ü ΓÇö α┤ëα┤▒α┤Üα╡ìα┤Üα╡ü α┤¬α┤┐α┤ƒα┤┐α┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤ò Quality cam_dark Too dark ΓÇö move to open shade α┤çα┤░α╡üα┤ƒα╡ìα┤ƒα┤╛α┤úα╡ì ΓÇö α┤ñα┤úα╡╜ α┤çα┤ƒα┤ñα╡ìα┤ñα╡ì α┤¿α┤┐α╡╜α┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤ò Quality cam_good Good ΓÇö tap to capture α┤¿α┤▓α╡ìα┤▓α┤ñα╡ì ΓÇö α┤½α╡ïα┤ƒα╡ìα┤ƒα╡ï α┤Äα┤ƒα╡üα┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤ò Offline offline_banner Working offline ΓÇö saved data shown α┤çα┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡╝α┤¿α╡åα┤▒α╡ìα┤▒α╡ì α┤çα┤▓α╡ìα┤▓ ΓÇö α┤╕α╡çα┤╡α╡ì α┤Üα╡åα┤»α╡ìα┤ñ α┤íα╡çα┤▒α╡ìα┤▒ Safety scheme_caveat Final eligibility determined by concerned authority α┤àα┤¿α╡ìα┤ñα┤┐α┤« α┤»α╡ïα┤ùα╡ìα┤»α┤ñ α┤¼α┤¿α╡ìα┤ºα┤¬α╡ìα┤¬α╡åα┤ƒα╡ìα┤ƒ α┤àα┤ºα┤┐α┤òα┤╛α┤░α┤┐ α┤¿α┤┐α╡╝α┤úα╡ìα┤úα┤»α┤┐α┤òα╡ìα┤òα╡üα┤é Errors err_no_internet No internet ΓÇö showing saved data α┤çα┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡╝α┤¿α╡åα┤▒α╡ìα┤▒α╡ì α┤çα┤▓α╡ìα┤▓ ΓÇö α┤╕α╡çα┤╡α╡ì α┤Üα╡åα┤»α╡ìα┤ñ α┤íα╡çα┤▒α╡ìα┤▒ Risk risk_disease Disease Risk α┤░α╡ïα┤ù α┤àα┤¬α┤òα┤ƒα┤é Risk risk_water Water Stress α┤£α┤▓ α┤¬α╡ìα┤░α┤ñα┤┐α┤╕α┤¿α╡ìα┤ºα┤┐ ΓÜá Do NOT use automated translation for agricultural and safety strings. All Malayalam strings for chemical recommendations, scheme eligibility, and confidence states must be reviewed by a native Malayalam speaker with agricultural knowledge before any release. AGRI-SAGE UI/UX SPECIFICATION v1.0 Malayalam-first ┬╖ Offline-ready ┬╖ Accessible ┬╖ Farmer-centered


=== 5_AgriSAGE_BackendSchema.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE BACKEND SCHEMA DOCUMENT Complete SQL DDL ┬╖ Pydantic Schemas ┬╖ Agent I/O Contracts ┬╖ LangGraph Definition ┬╖ API Contracts ┬╖ Migration Order Field Detail Document Backend Schema v1.0 Audience Backend Developer, VSCode AI Agent Database PostgreSQL 16 + pgvector 0.6+ ORM SQLAlchemy 2.0 async + Alembic migrations Validation Pydantic v2 throughout ΓÇö no dict passing between agents 1. DATABASE SCHEMA ΓÇö COMPLETE SQL DDL 1.1 Extensions &amp; Initial Setup -- Run once as superuser before any migrations CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS &quot;uuid-ossp&quot;; -- UUID generation CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS vector; -- pgvector CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS postgis; -- Geographic queries -- Performance tuning for Oracle Always-Free 1GB RAM instance -- Add to postgresql.conf: -- shared_buffers = 256MB -- effective_cache_size = 768MB -- work_mem = 4MB -- maintenance_work_mem = 64MB -- max_connections = 100 -- checkpoint_completion_target = 0.9 1.2 Users &amp; Authentication Tables CREATE TABLE users ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), phone VARCHAR(15) UNIQUE NOT NULL, name VARCHAR(100), language VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;ml&apos;, location_lat DECIMAL(9,6), location_lon DECIMAL(9,6), district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT &apos;Kerala&apos;, subscription_tier VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;free&apos;, subscription_expires TIMESTAMPTZ, trial_started_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), advisories_this_month INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, advisories_reset_at DATE NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_DATE, is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), last_active_at TIMESTAMPTZ ); CREATE TABLE otp_sessions ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), phone VARCHAR(15) NOT NULL, otp_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL, attempts INT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL, used BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_otp_phone ON otp_sessions(phone, expires_at); CREATE TABLE refresh_tokens ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, token_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL UNIQUE, expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL, revoked BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT false, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_refresh_user ON refresh_tokens(user_id) WHERE NOT revoked; 1.3 Farm, Field &amp; Soil Tables CREATE TABLE farms ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, location GEOMETRY(POINT, 4326), district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100), area_ha DECIMAL(8,2), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_farms_user ON farms(user_id); CREATE TABLE fields ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), farm_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES farms(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, name VARCHAR(100), boundaries GEOMETRY(POLYGON, 4326), area_ha DECIMAL(8,2), slope VARCHAR(20) CHECK (slope IN (&apos;flat&apos;,&apos;gentle&apos;,&apos;moderate&apos;,&apos;steep&apos;)), irrigation VARCHAR(30), soil_type VARCHAR(50), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_fields_farm ON fields(farm_id); CREATE TABLE soil_profiles ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), field_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, pH DECIMAL(4,2) CHECK (pH BETWEEN 0 AND 14), nitrogen_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), phosphorus_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), potassium_kgha DECIMAL(8,2), organic_carbon DECIMAL(5,2), texture VARCHAR(30), field_capacity DECIMAL(5,2), wilting_point DECIMAL(5,2), source VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT &apos;estimated&apos;, measured_at DATE, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); 1.4 Crop Cycles &amp; Observations CREATE TABLE crop_cycles ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), field_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fields(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, crop VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, cultivar VARCHAR(100), season VARCHAR(20), planting_date DATE NOT NULL, expected_harvest DATE, actual_harvest DATE, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;active&apos; CHECK (status IN (&apos;active&apos;,&apos;harvested&apos;,&apos;abandoned&apos;)), notes TEXT, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_cycles_field ON crop_cycles(field_id); CREATE INDEX idx_cycles_active ON crop_cycles(status) WHERE status = &apos;active&apos;; CREATE TABLE observations ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop_cycle_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crop_cycles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, location GEOMETRY(POINT, 4326), problem_description TEXT, voice_note_key VARCHAR(500), irrigation_status VARCHAR(20), fertilizer_recent BOOLEAN, pesticide_recent BOOLEAN, observed_growth_stage VARCHAR(50), observed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_obs_cycle ON observations(crop_cycle_id); CREATE INDEX idx_obs_time ON observations(observed_at DESC); CREATE TABLE images ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, r2_key VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL UNIQUE, mime_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, file_size_bytes INTEGER, width_px INTEGER, height_px INTEGER, quality_score DECIMAL(4,2) CHECK (quality_score BETWEEN 0 AND 1), blur_score DECIMAL(8,2), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_images_obs ON images(observation_id); 1.5 Diagnoses &amp; Advisories CREATE TABLE diagnoses ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, model_version VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, label VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, confidence_score DECIMAL(4,3) NOT NULL CHECK (confidence_score BETWEEN 0 AND 1), confidence_state VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, severity VARCHAR(20) CHECK (severity IN (&apos;low&apos;,&apos;medium&apos;,&apos;high&apos;,&apos;critical&apos;)), affected_area_pct DECIMAL(5,2), top_predictions JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE weather_snapshots ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID REFERENCES observations(id), lat DECIMAL(9,6) NOT NULL, lon DECIMAL(9,6) NOT NULL, temperature_c DECIMAL(5,2), humidity_pct DECIMAL(5,2), rainfall_24h_mm DECIMAL(8,2), wind_speed_kmh DECIMAL(6,2), dew_point_c DECIMAL(5,2), solar_rad_wm2 DECIMAL(8,2), consecutive_wet INTEGER, forecast_json JSONB, indicators_json JSONB, provider VARCHAR(50), snapshot_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_weather_obs ON weather_snapshots(observation_id); CREATE INDEX idx_weather_loc ON weather_snapshots(lat, lon, snapshot_at DESC); CREATE TABLE advisories ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), observation_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES observations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, confidence_state VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL, recommended_scenario VARCHAR(5), scenarios_json JSONB NOT NULL, evidence_bundle_json JSONB NOT NULL, recommended_action_json JSONB NOT NULL, what_would_change_json JSONB, safe_to_act BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT true, sources_json JSONB, scheme_matches_json JSONB, model_version VARCHAR(50), knowledge_version VARCHAR(50), generation_ms INTEGER, langgraph_trace_id VARCHAR(100), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_adv_obs ON advisories(observation_id); CREATE INDEX idx_adv_created ON advisories(created_at DESC); 1.6 Interventions &amp; Outcomes CREATE TABLE interventions ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop_cycle_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES crop_cycles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, advisory_id UUID REFERENCES advisories(id), action_type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, description TEXT, product_used VARCHAR(200), quantity DECIMAL(10,3), quantity_unit VARCHAR(20), cost_inr DECIMAL(10,2), performed_at DATE NOT NULL, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE outcomes ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), intervention_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES interventions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, observed_at DATE, improved VARCHAR(10) CHECK (improved IN (&apos;yes&apos;,&apos;no&apos;,&apos;partial&apos;,&apos;unsure&apos;)), notes TEXT, follow_up_image UUID REFERENCES images(id), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); 1.7 Simulation CREATE TABLE simulation_runs ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), advisory_id UUID REFERENCES advisories(id), simulator VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CHECK (simulator IN (&apos;dssat&apos;,&apos;apsim&apos;,&apos;rules&apos;)), crop VARCHAR(50), input_json JSONB NOT NULL, output_json JSONB, error_message TEXT, runtime_ms INTEGER, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); 1.8 Knowledge Base Tables CREATE TABLE knowledge_documents ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), title VARCHAR(500), crop VARCHAR(50), state VARCHAR(100), district VARCHAR(100), topic VARCHAR(100), growth_stage VARCHAR(50), source_tier INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK (source_tier BETWEEN 1 AND 6), source_authority VARCHAR(200), source_url VARCHAR(1000), language VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;en&apos;, published_at DATE, valid_until DATE NOT NULL, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;PUBLISHED&apos; CHECK (status IN (&apos;DISCOVERED&apos;,&apos;PARSED&apos;,&apos;VALIDATED&apos;,&apos;APPROVED&apos;,&apos;PUBLISHED&apos;,&apos;SUPERSEDED&apos;,&apos;ARCHIVED&apos;)), content_hash CHAR(64), version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1, license VARCHAR(100), created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE knowledge_chunks ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), document_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES knowledge_documents(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, chunk_index INTEGER NOT NULL, content TEXT NOT NULL, embedding vector(1024), metadata JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), UNIQUE(document_id, chunk_index) ); -- IVFFlat ANN index ΓÇö rebuild after major bulk loads CREATE INDEX idx_chunks_embedding ON knowledge_chunks USING ivfflat (embedding vector_cosine_ops) WITH (lists = 100); CREATE INDEX idx_chunks_doc ON knowledge_chunks(document_id); -- Filter index for freshness checks CREATE INDEX idx_docs_valid ON knowledge_documents(valid_until, status) WHERE status = &apos;PUBLISHED&apos;; 1.9 Schemes &amp; Market CREATE TABLE schemes ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), name VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL, short_name VARCHAR(50), authority VARCHAR(200), authority_level VARCHAR(20) CHECK (authority_level IN (&apos;central&apos;,&apos;state&apos;,&apos;district&apos;)), state VARCHAR(100), crops_applicable JSONB, eligibility_rules JSONB NOT NULL, benefits_json JSONB, enrollment_url VARCHAR(500), helpline VARCHAR(50), valid_from DATE, valid_until DATE NOT NULL, status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;active&apos;, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_schemes_valid ON schemes(valid_until, status) WHERE status=&apos;active&apos;; CREATE TABLE market_prices ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), crop VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, market_name VARCHAR(200) NOT NULL, district VARCHAR(100), state VARCHAR(100), price_date DATE NOT NULL, min_price DECIMAL(10,2), modal_price DECIMAL(10,2), max_price DECIMAL(10,2), unit VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;quintal&apos;, source VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL DEFAULT &apos;enam&apos;, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(), UNIQUE(crop, market_name, price_date) ); CREATE INDEX idx_market_crop_date ON market_prices(crop, price_date DESC); 1.10 Notifications, Expert Reviews &amp; Audit CREATE TABLE notifications ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE, farm_id UUID REFERENCES farms(id), type VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, severity VARCHAR(20) CHECK (severity IN (&apos;info&apos;,&apos;watch&apos;,&apos;attention&apos;,&apos;action&apos;,&apos;urgent&apos;)), title VARCHAR(300) NOT NULL, body TEXT, data_json JSONB, fcm_message_id VARCHAR(200), delivered_at TIMESTAMPTZ, opened_at TIMESTAMPTZ, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_notif_user ON notifications(user_id, created_at DESC); CREATE TABLE expert_reviews ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), advisory_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES advisories(id), expert_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES users(id), verdict VARCHAR(20) CHECK (verdict IN (&apos;agree&apos;,&apos;disagree&apos;,&apos;partial&apos;)), safety_ok BOOLEAN, notes TEXT, corrections JSONB, reviewed_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE TABLE audit_logs ( id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(), user_id UUID REFERENCES users(id), entity_type VARCHAR(50), entity_id UUID, action VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL, ip_address INET, user_agent TEXT, details_json JSONB, created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW() ); CREATE INDEX idx_audit_user ON audit_logs(user_id, created_at DESC); CREATE INDEX idx_audit_entity ON audit_logs(entity_type, entity_id, created_at DESC); 1.11 Row-Level Security Policies -- Enable RLS ALTER TABLE farms ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE fields ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE crop_cycles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE observations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE images ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE advisories ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE interventions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE outcomes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; ALTER TABLE notifications ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY; -- Users see only their own farms CREATE POLICY farms_owner ON farms USING (user_id = current_setting(&apos;app.current_user_id&apos;)::UUID); -- Fields inherit farm ownership CREATE POLICY fields_owner ON fields USING (farm_id IN ( SELECT id FROM farms WHERE user_id = current_setting(&apos;app.current_user_id&apos;)::UUID )); -- Notifications visible to owner CREATE POLICY notifications_owner ON notifications USING (user_id = current_setting(&apos;app.current_user_id&apos;)::UUID); -- Service role bypasses RLS (for background workers) -- Set via: SET ROLE agrisage_service; 2. PYDANTIC SCHEMAS ΓÇö ALL AGENT I/O 2.1 Farm State Schema (Agent Input) from pydantic import BaseModel, Field from typing import Optional, List from datetime import date, datetime from enum import Enum class CropEnum(str, Enum): RICE = &apos;rice&apos; BANANA = &apos;banana&apos; COCONUT = &apos;coconut&apos; TOMATO = &apos;tomato&apos; MAIZE = &apos;maize&apos; class ConfidenceState(str, Enum): CONFIRMED = &apos;CONFIRMED&apos; LIKELY = &apos;LIKELY&apos; POSSIBLE = &apos;POSSIBLE&apos; UNCERTAIN = &apos;UNCERTAIN&apos; INSUFFICIENT_DATA = &apos;INSUFFICIENT_DATA&apos; CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE = &apos;CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE&apos; OUT_OF_SCOPE = &apos;OUT_OF_SCOPE&apos; class LocationSchema(BaseModel): lat: float = Field(..., ge=-90, le=90) lon: float = Field(..., ge=-180, le=180) district: Optional[str] = None state: str = &apos;Kerala&apos; class FarmStateSchema(BaseModel): observation_id: str crop: CropEnum cultivar: Optional[str] = None location: LocationSchema planting_date: date days_after_sowing: int observed_growth_stage: Optional[str] = None irrigation_status: Optional[str] = None fertilizer_recent: Optional[bool] = None pesticide_recent: Optional[bool] = None problem_description: Optional[str] = None image_ids: List[str] = [] language: str = &apos;ml&apos; farmer_budget_inr: Optional[float] = None timestamp: datetime 2.2 Agent Output Schemas class VisionResult(BaseModel): label: str confidence_score: float = Field(..., ge=0, le=1) severity: Optional[str] = None affected_area_pct: Optional[float] = None top_predictions: List[dict] # [{label, score}] quality_score: float out_of_distribution: bool model_version: str class WeatherIndicators(BaseModel): disease_favorability_index: float # 0.0-1.0 spray_window_available: bool spray_window_time: Optional[str] = None # e.g. &apos;07:00-10:00&apos; irrigation_recommendation: str # skip/delay/proceed heat_stress_risk: str # low/medium/high/critical flood_risk: str consecutive_wet_days: int class WeatherResult(BaseModel): temperature_c: float humidity_pct: float rainfall_24h_mm: float wind_speed_kmh: float forecast_7day: List[dict] indicators: WeatherIndicators provider: str snapshot_at: datetime is_cached: bool cache_age_minutes: Optional[int] = None class CropStageResult(BaseModel): stage_name: str stage_code: str days_after_sowing: int gdd_accumulated: float gdd_total_required: float disease_vulnerability: str # low/medium/high upcoming_windows: List[str] # e.g. [&apos;Flowering in 8-12 days&apos;] confidence: float class EvidenceItem(BaseModel): type: str # vision/weather/crop_stage/knowledge/simulation label: str value: Optional[str] = None supports_diagnosis: bool confidence: Optional[float] = None source_tier: Optional[int] = None class KnowledgeResult(BaseModel): chunks: List[dict] # retrieved chunks sources: List[dict] # [{title, authority, url, tier}] highest_tier: int retrieval_score: float class CriticResult(BaseModel): confidence_state: ConfidenceState confidence_score: float conflicts: List[str] missing_data: List[dict] # [{field, impact}] safe_to_act: bool run_simulation: bool safety_blocks: List[str] # blocked recommendations class ScenarioResult(BaseModel): id: str # A/B/C label: str action: str timing: str expected_yield_kgha: Optional[float] = None # MODELLED expected_cost_inr: Optional[float] = None expected_net_return_inr: Optional[float] = None risk_level: str confidence: float class AdvisoryResponse(BaseModel): advisory_id: str observation_id: str confidence_state: ConfidenceState diagnosis_label: str diagnosis_label_local: dict # {ml, ta, en} confidence_score: float severity: Optional[str] = None affected_area_pct: Optional[float] = None risk_level: str risk_horizon_days: Optional[int] = None scenarios: List[ScenarioResult] recommended_scenario: str recommended_action: dict evidence_items: List[EvidenceItem] contradictions: List[str] missing_data: List[dict] what_would_change_this: List[str] safe_to_act: bool scheme_matches: List[dict] sources: List[dict] model_version: str knowledge_version: str generation_ms: int created_at: datetime 3. LANGGRAPH GRAPH DEFINITION 3.1 State Definition from langgraph.graph import StateGraph, END from typing import TypedDict, Optional, List, Annotated import operator class AgriSAGEState(TypedDict): # Input farm_state: FarmStateSchema images: List[bytes] # Agent outputs vision_result: Optional[VisionResult] weather_result: Optional[WeatherResult] crop_stage_result: Optional[CropStageResult] knowledge_result: Optional[KnowledgeResult] critic_result: Optional[CriticResult] scheme_result: Optional[dict] simulation_result: Optional[dict] decision_result: Optional[dict] # Final advisory: Optional[AdvisoryResponse] # Control errors: Annotated[List[str], operator.add] warnings: Annotated[List[str], operator.add] run_simulation: bool 3.2 Graph Wiring def build_advisory_graph() -&gt; StateGraph: graph = StateGraph(AgriSAGEState) # Add all nodes graph.add_node(&apos;intake&apos;, intake_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;vision&apos;, vision_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;weather&apos;, weather_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;crop_stage&apos;, crop_stage_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;knowledge&apos;, knowledge_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;critic&apos;, critic_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;scheme&apos;, scheme_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;simulation&apos;, simulation_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;decision&apos;, decision_agent.run) graph.add_node(&apos;explanation&apos;, explanation_agent.run) # Entry point graph.set_entry_point(&apos;intake&apos;) # Intake ΓåÆ parallel execution (vision + weather + stage + knowledge) graph.add_edge(&apos;intake&apos;, &apos;vision&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;intake&apos;, &apos;weather&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;intake&apos;, &apos;crop_stage&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;intake&apos;, &apos;knowledge&apos;) # All 4 parallel ΓåÆ critic (barrier node) graph.add_edge(&apos;vision&apos;, &apos;critic&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;weather&apos;, &apos;critic&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;crop_stage&apos;, &apos;critic&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;knowledge&apos;, &apos;critic&apos;) # Critic ΓåÆ scheme (always) + simulation (conditional) graph.add_edge(&apos;critic&apos;, &apos;scheme&apos;) graph.add_conditional_edges( &apos;critic&apos;, lambda s: &apos;simulation&apos; if s[&apos;run_simulation&apos;] else &apos;decision&apos;, {&apos;simulation&apos;: &apos;simulation&apos;, &apos;decision&apos;: &apos;decision&apos;} ) # Simulation ΓåÆ decision graph.add_edge(&apos;simulation&apos;, &apos;decision&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;scheme&apos;, &apos;decision&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;decision&apos;, &apos;explanation&apos;) graph.add_edge(&apos;explanation&apos;, END) return graph.compile() advisory_graph = build_advisory_graph() 4. ABSTRACTION INTERFACES 4.1 Weather Provider from abc import ABC, abstractmethod class WeatherProvider(ABC): @abstractmethod async def get_current(self, lat: float, lon: float) -&gt; WeatherSnapshot: ... @abstractmethod async def get_forecast(self, lat: float, lon: float, days: int) -&gt; List[WeatherForecast]: ... @abstractmethod async def get_historical(self, lat: float, lon: float, start: date, end: date) -&gt; List[WeatherDay]: ... class OpenMeteoProvider(WeatherProvider): # Development only ΓÇö non-commercial BASE_URL = &apos;https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast&apos; # Implementation... class IMDProvider(WeatherProvider): # Production ΓÇö commercial API # Implementation... class CachedWeatherProvider(WeatherProvider): def __init__(self, provider: WeatherProvider, ttl_minutes: int = 30): self.provider = provider self.ttl = ttl_minutes # Caches in memory + falls back to DB cache 4.2 Simulation Engine class SimulationEngine(ABC): @abstractmethod async def simulate(self, params: SimulationParams) -&gt; SimulationResult: ... @abstractmethod def get_supported_crops(self) -&gt; List[str]: ... @abstractmethod def get_required_inputs(self) -&gt; dict: ... class DSSATEngine(SimulationEngine): # BSD-3-Clause ΓÇö production safe async def simulate(self, params: SimulationParams) -&gt; SimulationResult: # 1. Validate params against DSSAT schema # 2. Write DSSAT input files to temp directory # 3. subprocess.run([&apos;dscsm048&apos;, &apos;B&apos;, &apos;DSSBatch.v48&apos;]) # 4. Parse .OUT files # 5. Return structured SimulationResult ... class LocalRulesEngine(SimulationEngine): # Offline fallback ΓÇö deterministic rule-based # No subprocess ΓÇö pure Python async def simulate(self, params: SimulationParams) -&gt; SimulationResult: # Rule-based yield estimation from crop stage + weather + interventions ... 4.3 Speech Provider class SpeechProvider(ABC): @abstractmethod async def transcribe(self, audio: bytes, language: str) -&gt; str: ... @abstractmethod async def synthesize(self, text: str, language: str) -&gt; bytes: ... @abstractmethod def get_supported_languages(self) -&gt; List[str]: ... class BhashiniProvider(SpeechProvider): # Apply at: bhashini.gov.in ΓÇö 4-6 week approval ... class AndroidTTSFallback(SpeechProvider): # Used when Bhashini unavailable ΓÇö Android built-in # Limited language support but no API dependency ... 5. ALEMBIC MIGRATION ORDER 5.1 Migration Sequence Migration File Creates Depends On 001_extensions.py uuid-ossp, vector, postgis extensions None ΓÇö first migration always 002_users.py users, otp_sessions, refresh_tokens 001 003_farms.py farms, fields, soil_profiles 002 004_crop_cycles.py crop_cycles 003 005_observations.py observations, images 004 006_weather.py weather_snapshots 005 007_diagnoses.py diagnoses 005 008_advisories.py advisories 007 009_interventions.py interventions, outcomes 008 010_simulation.py simulation_runs 008 011_knowledge.py knowledge_documents, knowledge_chunks, IVFFlat index 001 012_schemes.py schemes 001 013_market.py market_prices 001 014_notifications.py notifications 002 015_expert.py expert_reviews 008, 002 016_audit.py audit_logs 002 017_rls.py All RLS policies 002-016 complete ΓÜá Never run migrations out of order. The IVFFlat index in 011 requires pgvector to be installed (001). RLS policies in 017 must run LAST ΓÇö after all tables exist. 5.2 Running Migrations # Initial setup alembic init alembic # Configure alembic.ini with DATABASE_URL # Run all migrations alembic upgrade head # Check current state alembic current # Rollback one step alembic downgrade -1 # Generate new migration after schema change alembic revision --autogenerate -m &apos;description&apos; 6. SAFETY CHECK IMPLEMENTATIONS 6.1 Chemical Safety Check class ChemicalSafetyChecker: &quot;&quot;&quot; All chemical recommendations must pass these checks. A BLOCK result means the advisory is held ΓÇö not shown to farmer. &quot;&quot;&quot; async def check_product_exists(self, product_name: str) -&gt; bool: &quot;&quot;&quot;Product name must exist in CIB&amp;RC database.&quot;&quot;&quot; result = await db.fetch_one( &apos;SELECT id FROM chemical_labels WHERE product_name ILIKE :name&apos;, {&apos;name&apos;: product_name} ) return result is not None async def check_crop_registration(self, product_name: str, crop: str) -&gt; bool: &quot;&quot;&quot;Product must be registered for use on stated crop.&quot;&quot;&quot; result = await db.fetch_one( &apos;SELECT id FROM chemical_labels WHERE product_name ILIKE :name AND :crop = ANY(registered_crops)&apos;, {&apos;name&apos;: product_name, &apos;crop&apos;: crop} ) return result is not None async def check_dosage(self, product_name: str, stated_dose: float, unit: str, crop: str) -&gt; bool: &quot;&quot;&quot;Stated dosage must be within label-approved range.&quot;&quot;&quot; label = await self.get_label(product_name, crop) if label is None: return False return label.min_dose &lt;= stated_dose &lt;= label.max_dose async def run_all_checks(self, recommendation: dict) -&gt; tuple[bool, List[str]]: &quot;&quot;&quot;Run all safety checks. Returns (safe, list_of_issues).&quot;&quot;&quot; issues = [] product = recommendation.get(&apos;product_name&apos;) if not product: return True, [] # No chemical ΓÇö skip if not await self.check_product_exists(product): issues.append(f&apos;BLOCK: Product {product} not found in CIB&amp;RC database&apos;) if not await self.check_crop_registration(product, recommendation[&apos;crop&apos;]): issues.append(f&apos;BLOCK: {product} not registered for {recommendation[&quot;crop&quot;]}&apos;) if recommendation.get(&apos;dosage&apos;): if not await self.check_dosage(product, recommendation[&apos;dosage&apos;], recommendation[&apos;unit&apos;], recommendation[&apos;crop&apos;]): issues.append(f&apos;BLOCK: Dosage outside label-approved range&apos;) return len(issues) == 0, issues AGRI-SAGE BACKEND SCHEMA v1.0 Complete SQL DDL ┬╖ Pydantic Schemas ┬╖ LangGraph Definition ┬╖ Safety Checks

AGRI-SAGE IMPLEMENTATION PLAN Zero-Cost Build ┬╖ VSCode AI Agent ┬╖ Phase-by-Phase with Daily Tasks Field Detail Document Implementation Plan v1.0 Audience Developer (BeginnerΓÇôIntermediate), VSCode AI Agent Build Cost Zero ΓÇö 100% free/open-source tools and hosting Timeline 52 weeks ΓÇö 6 phases ΓÇö MVP at Week 16 Execution VSCode + GitHub Copilot / Cursor AI Agent Revenue Start Freemium subscription after closed beta (Week 48+) HOW TO USE THIS DOCUMENT This document is the single source of truth for building Agri-SAGE from zero. Every task is specific enough for a VSCode AI agent to execute. Work through phases in order. Never skip a phase. Each phase has a clear exit criteria ΓÇö do not proceed until all exit criteria pass. Each task is a VSCode AI agent prompt or a manual step ΓÇö labelled clearly Code blocks show exact commands to run in terminal Exit criteria at the end of each phase must ALL pass before moving to next phase Free-tier services are used throughout ΓÇö commercial alternatives noted where needed at scale ΓÜá Read the entire LICENSE_REGISTRY.md (created in Phase 0 Task 3) before writing any code. APSIM requires a separate commercial license for revenue-generating use. Open-Meteo free API is non-commercial. These constraints affect architecture decisions. PHASE 0: FOUNDATION (Weeks 1ΓÇô2) Goal: Repository structure, development environment, database running, CI/CD pipeline. No features yet ΓÇö only the foundation everything else builds on. Phase 1 ΓÇö Task List Task 1.1: All Remaining Database Migrations VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create Alembic migrations 003 through 016 for Agri-SAGE using the exact SQL DDL from Backend Schema Document Section 1.3 through 1.10. Task 1.3: Auth API Endpoints VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/api/v1/auth.py for Agri-SAGE with these endpoints: POST /api/v1/auth/send-otp (accepts phone, generates 6-digit OTP, hashes with bcrypt, stores in otp_sessions with 5-minute expiry, calls OTP provider ΓÇö use stub that prints OTP to console in development), POST /api/v1/auth/verify-otp (accepts phone+otp, verifies hash, checks expiry, checks attempts&lt;5, creates or retrieves user, returns JWT access_token + refresh_token), POST /api/v1/auth/refresh (accepts refresh_token, validates, rotates token), POST /api/v1/auth/logout (revokes refresh_token). Use python-jose for JWT with HS256. Access token expiry 15 minutes. Refresh token expiry 7 days. Rate limit: max 5 OTP attempts per phone per 10 minutes. Task 1.4: Farm &amp; Field CRUD VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/api/v1/farms.py for Agri-SAGE with full CRUD: POST /api/v1/farms (create farm ΓÇö requires JWT ΓÇö set app.current_user_id in DB session for RLS), GET /api/v1/farms (list user&apos;s farms), GET /api/v1/farms/{id} (get single farm), PUT /api/v1/farms/{id} (update), DELETE /api/v1/farms/{id} (soft delete). POST /api/v1/farms/{id}/fields, GET/PUT/DELETE for fields. All endpoints: validate JWT, set PostgreSQL session variable for RLS, use Pydantic v2 request/response schemas, return 404 if resource not found or not owned by user. Task 1.5: Image Upload Endpoint VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/api/v1/observations.py for Agri-SAGE. POST /api/v1/observations accepts multipart form: crop_cycle_id (str), description (str optional), images (List[UploadFile] max 5). For each image: validate MIME type is image/jpeg or image/png only, validate size &lt; settings.MAX_IMAGE_SIZE_MB MB, strip EXIF metadata using Pillow, resize to max 1200px longest edge preserving aspect ratio, generate UUID filename, upload to Cloudflare R2 using boto3 with R2 endpoint URL. Store observation and image records in DB. Return observation_id and list of image_ids. Use httpx for R2 uploads or boto3 with endpoint_url=&apos;https://ACCOUNT_ID.r2.cloudflarestorage.com&apos;. Task 1.6: Vision Model Training VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/ml/vision/train.py for Agri-SAGE. Training script for EfficientNet-B3 disease classifier. Uses torchvision.models.efficientnet_b3 with pretrained=True. Final classifier replaced with Linear(1536, num_classes). Training: Adam optimizer lr=1e-4, CosineAnnealingLR scheduler, CrossEntropyLoss with class weights for imbalance. Augmentation pipeline: RandomCrop(300), RandomHorizontalFlip, ColorJitter(brightness=0.3, contrast=0.3), GaussianBlur(p=0.2), Normalize(ImageNet stats). Evaluation: macro-F1, per-class precision/recall, confusion matrix. FARMER-LEVEL train/val/test split ΓÇö never random split on image level. Post-training: temperature scaling calibration targeting ECE &lt; 0.10. Save best model checkpoint and calibrated model separately. Task 1.7: ONNX Export VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/ml/vision/export.py for Agri-SAGE. Function export_to_onnx(model_path, output_path): loads calibrated PyTorch model, sets eval(), runs torch.onnx.export with opset_version=17, input_names=[&apos;image&apos;], output_names=[&apos;logits&apos;], dynamic_axes for batch. Function quantize_int8(onnx_path, quantized_path): uses onnxruntime.quantization.quantize_dynamic with QInt8 weight type. Function verify_accuracy_loss(fp32_path, int8_path, test_loader): runs both models on test set, asserts macro-F1 difference &lt; 0.02. Script main() runs all three steps and prints final size and accuracy metrics. Task 1.8: Vision Service VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/services/vision_service.py for Agri-SAGE. Class VisionService with: __init__ loads ONNX Runtime session from model file path (uses CUDAExecutionProvider if GPU available, fallback CPUExecutionProvider), preprocess_image(image_bytes) resizes to 300x300 and normalizes to ImageNet stats returning numpy array, check_image_quality(image_bytes) computes Laplacian variance for blur + mean brightness for lighting + estimates crop region coverage ΓÇö returns QualityResult with score and specific failure reason, classify(image_bytes) runs full pipeline: quality check ΓåÆ if fails return INSUFFICIENT_DATA with message ΓåÆ else run ONNX inference ΓåÆ apply temperature scaling ΓåÆ return VisionResult with top-5 predictions, confidence state, severity. All crop class names loaded from JSON file. Task 1.9: Intake &amp; Critic Agents VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/intake.py: IntakeAgent.run(state) merges raw request data with stored farm profile from DB, validates and fills all FarmStateSchema fields, computes days_after_sowing from planting_date, returns updated state. Create backend/agents/critic.py: CriticAgent.run(state) implements all 8 safety checks from TRD Section 5.3 ΓÇö check_chemical_in_cibrc_database, check_dosage_within_label_range, check_crop_chemical_registration, check_preharvest_interval, check_reentry_interval, check_scheme_exists_in_database, check_document_not_expired, check_no_llm_invented_products. Runs conflict detection between vision/weather/stage results. Computes confidence_state using ConfidenceState enum. Sets run_simulation=True only if confidence_score &gt; 0.65 AND no conflicts AND confidence_state in [CONFIRMED, LIKELY]. Task 1.10: Basic Diagnose Endpoint VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/api/v1/diagnose.py for Agri-SAGE. POST /api/v1/diagnose accepts {observation_id: str}, requires JWT. Flow: load observation + images from DB, validate ownership via RLS, run advisory_graph (LangGraph) with farm state, record timing, save advisory to DB, return full AdvisoryResponse schema. For MVP: Vision agent runs ONNX locally, Weather agent calls Open-Meteo (development), Crop Stage agent computes GDD, Knowledge agent returns cached placeholder text, Critic agent runs all safety checks. No simulation in MVP. Return advisory even if some agents return partial results ΓÇö mark clearly in confidence_state. Task 1.13: Android Room Database VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create Room database for Agri-SAGE Android app. Entities: FarmerEntity (id, phone, name, language, subscriptionTier, updatedAt), FarmEntity (id, userId, name, locationLat, locationLon, district, state, areaHa, isSynced, createdAt), FieldEntity, CropCycleEntity (id, fieldId, crop, cultivar, plantingDate, status, isSynced), ObservationEntity (id, cropCycleId, problemDescription, observedAt, isSynced), CachedAdvisoryEntity (id, observationId, advisoryJson, createdAt), WeatherCacheEntity (lat, lon, dataJson, cachedAt), SyncQueueEntity (id autoGenerate, entityType, entityId, action, payloadJson, priority, retryCount, createdAt). This is the MVP milestone ΓÇö a complete, usable product. Phase 2 ΓÇö Task List Task 2.1: Weather Agent VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/weather.py for Agri-SAGE. WeatherAgent.run(state) calls WeatherProvider to get current conditions and 7-day forecast for farm location. Derive ALL agricultural indicators: DFI (Disease Favorability Index) = weighted average of (humidity &gt; 80% score) + (temperature 20-30┬░C score) + (consecutive_wet_days score), clamped 0-1. SprayWindow: rain probability &lt; 20% AND wind &lt; 15 km/h for any 4-hour block today ΓÇö return best window time. IrrigationRecommendation: if rain &gt; 10mm expected in 24hrs ΓåÆ &apos;skip&apos;, if soil dry estimate AND no rain ΓåÆ &apos;proceed&apos;, else &apos;delay&apos;. HeatStressRisk: based on crop-specific temperature thresholds. Return WeatherResult with all indicators. Cache by (lat, lon) rounded to 2dp with 30-minute TTL using in-memory dict. Task 2.2: Crop Stage Agent VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/crop_stage.py for Agri-SAGE. CropStageAgent.run(state) computes phenological stage using Growing Degree Days (GDD). GDD formula: ((Tmax + Tmin) / 2) - Tbase where Tbase is crop-specific (rice=10┬░C, maize=10┬░C, tomato=10┬░C, banana=15┬░C, coconut=20┬░C). Load historical weather from DB to compute cumulative GDD from planting_date to today. Compare against crop-cultivar GDD thresholds table (JSON file per crop defining: germination, seedling, tillering, panicle_initiation, flowering, grain_fill, maturity with GDD values). Return CropStageResult with: stage_name, gdd_accumulated, gdd_total_required (to maturity), disease_vulnerability (per stage-disease mapping JSON), upcoming_windows (next stage and estimated days). If farmer provided observed_growth_stage, use that to correct GDD estimate. Task 2.3: Knowledge Ingestion Pipeline VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/knowledge/ingestion.py for Agri-SAGE. DocumentIngestionPipeline class with: fetch_document(url) downloads PDF/HTML using httpx, parse_pdf(content) extracts text preserving tables using PyMuPDF (fitz), parse_html(content) extracts text using BeautifulSoup, extract_metadata(text, url) uses regex patterns to find crop mentions, state mentions, publication date, extract_chunks(text, metadata) does hierarchical chunking ΓÇö split at heading boundaries first (H1/H2/H3 patterns), then at paragraph boundaries, max 512 tokens per chunk, min 100 tokens, preserve table rows intact, embed_chunks(chunks) calls multilingual-e5-large embedding model with &apos;passage: &apos; prefix per e5 convention, store_chunks(document_id, chunks, embeddings) bulk inserts into knowledge_chunks table. Script seed_knowledge.py: for each document URL in a seed_urls.json file, run the full pipeline. Task 2.4: Hybrid RAG Retrieval VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/knowledge/retrieval.py for Agri-SAGE. HybridRetriever class: dense_search(query_text, crop, state, top_k=15) embeds query with &apos;query: &apos; prefix using multilingual-e5-large, queries pgvector using SELECT ... ORDER BY embedding &lt;=&gt; query_vec WHERE crop=? AND status=&apos;PUBLISHED&apos; AND valid_until &gt; NOW() LIMIT top_k. sparse_search(keywords, crop, state, top_k=15) uses PostgreSQL full-text search: to_tsvector(&apos;english&apos;, content) @@ plainto_tsquery(:keywords). reciprocal_rank_fusion(dense_hits, sparse_hits, k=60) merges by RRF formula: score = sum(1/(k+rank)) per document. rerank(query, merged_hits, top_k=5) uses cross-encoder/ms-marco-MiniLM-L-6-v2 from sentence-transformers to score each chunk against query. apply_authority_boost(reranked): Tier 1 documents multiply score by 1.3. Return top_k chunks with source metadata. Task 2.5: Knowledge Agent VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/knowledge.py for Agri-SAGE. KnowledgeAgent.run(state) builds query from: crop name, disease label from vision_result, growth stage from crop_stage_result, state/district from location, problem_description. Calls HybridRetriever.retrieve() with metadata filters. Validates all returned chunks have valid_until &gt; today and status=PUBLISHED ΓÇö filters stale chunks silently. Returns KnowledgeResult with chunks, sources list, highest_tier found. If no Tier 1-2 sources found for a recommendation topic, sets a warning in state. Task 2.6: LangGraph Orchestration VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/orchestrator.py for Agri-SAGE implementing the complete LangGraph graph as defined in Backend Schema Document Section 3.2. Use asyncio-compatible LangGraph. Intake node runs first. Vision + Weather + CropStage + Knowledge nodes run in parallel using asyncio.gather() ΓÇö LangGraph fan-out. Critic node is barrier ΓÇö waits for all 4. After Critic: Scheme node always runs (async). Simulation node runs only if state[&apos;run_simulation&apos;]=True ΓÇö conditional edge. Decision + Explanation nodes run sequentially last. Total timeout: 10 seconds ΓÇö if any agent exceeds 3 seconds individually, log warning and continue with partial result. Store langgraph_trace_id in advisory record for debugging. Task 2.7: Decision &amp; Explanation Agents VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/decision.py: DecisionAgent scores each scenario using: yield_score (normalized expected yield), profit_score (expected net return), risk_penalty (risk_level: critical=-3, high=-2, medium=-1, low=0), water_penalty (irrigation cost), uncertainty_penalty (1-confidence_score * 2). Recommends highest scoring scenario. Returns DecisionResult with all 3 scenarios scored and ranked. Create backend/agents/explanation.py: ExplanationAgent builds advisory text using Qwen3-7B (server) or Qwen3-1.7B (offline). System prompt enforces: only use evidence bundle facts, label all yield as modelled estimate, use confidence_state to gate recommendations (UNCERTAIN ΓåÆ no treatment recommended), output structured JSON matching AdvisoryTextSchema. Uses LLM at temperature=0.2. Validates LLM output against schema before returning ΓÇö retries once if invalid JSON. Task 3.2: Scenario Engine VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/simulation/scenarios.py for Agri-SAGE. Task 3.3: Scheme Agent VSCode AI Agent Prompt: Γ£ô Create backend/agents/scheme.py for Agri-SAGE. SchemeAgent.run(state): (1) Load farmer profile (state, district, crop, land_area from farm). (2) Query schemes table: SELECT * FROM schemes WHERE :crop = ANY(crops_applicable) AND (state = :state OR state IS NULL) AND valid_until &gt; NOW() AND status=&apos;active&apos;. (3) For each scheme: run eligibility_rules JSON against farmer profile ΓÇö eligibility_rules is a JSON structure like {land_ha_max: 5, crops: [&apos;rice&apos;], state: &apos;Kerala&apos;, requires_soil_card: false}. (4) Score eligibility: 1.0=all criteria met, 0.5=most met, 0.0=fails mandatory criteria. (5) Return top 3 matches. ALWAYS include caveat: &apos;Final eligibility determined by concerned authority&apos; ΓÇö this is mandatory. NEVER return a scheme with valid_until in the past. EconomicsAgent.run(state): takes SimulationResult + MarketResult + farmer_budget_inr. For each scenario A/B/C: revenue = scenario.expected_yield_kgha * field.area_ha * market.current_modal_price_per_kg, input_cost = sum of fertilizer_events costs + pesticide costs + irrigation_cost_estimate, labour_cost = action_type_to_labour_days[action] * local_labour_rate_per_day, transport_cost = haversine(farm, nearest_market) * 2 * transport_rate_per_km_per_quintal * yield_quintals, net_return = revenue - input_cost - labour_cost - transport_cost.
