=== 7_AgriSAGE_Security_Compliance.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE SECURITY &amp; COMPLIANCE DOCUMENT API Security ┬╖ Data Privacy ┬╖ OWASP Checklist ┬╖ Audit Rules ┬╖ Privacy Policy Template ┬╖ Compliance Requirements Field Detail Document Security &amp; Compliance v1.0 Audience All developers ΓÇö every line of code must comply with this document Frameworks OWASP Mobile Top 10 ┬╖ OWASP API Security Top 10 ┬╖ India PDPB 2023 Enforcement Automated checks in CI/CD ΓÇö build fails on security violations Review Every new endpoint and agent must be reviewed against this document before merge 1. SECURITY ARCHITECTURE PRINCIPLES ΓÜá These are not guidelines ΓÇö they are hard rules. Any code that violates these rules must not be merged. The VSCode AI agent building this project must treat every rule in this document as a non-negotiable constraint. 1.1 The 10 Hard Security Rules Rule Statement How to Enforce SEC-001 Never store passwords. Use OTP-only authentication. No password field in users table. No bcrypt on user passwords. bcrypt used only for OTP hashes. SEC-002 Never hardcode secrets in source code. grep CI check: any pattern like API_KEY=&quot;...&quot; or secret = &quot;...&quot; with literal value fails build. SEC-003 User input is never trusted as a system instruction. All user text is treated as data. Never inserted into SQL strings. Never passed as LLM system prompt. SEC-004 Never expose internal error details to the client. All 500 errors return generic message. Error details logged server-side only. Never in API response. SEC-005 Every API endpoint requires authentication except /health and /auth/*. FastAPI dependency require_auth injected on every router. Verified in CI by scanning router definitions. SEC-006 Row-Level Security enforced at database level ΓÇö not only application level. PostgreSQL RLS policies applied. Application-level checks are secondary defense only. SEC-007 All file uploads validated for type, size, and content ΓÇö not just extension. MIME type from python-magic library. Size checked before reading. Metadata stripped before storage. SEC-008 LLM output is validated against schema before use. Never trusted raw. Explanation agent output parsed as JSON. Invalid JSON triggers retry. Fails to safe fallback after 2 retries. SEC-009 Simulation engine receives only validated typed structs. Never free text. SimulationParams Pydantic model validation before any DSSAT call. Schema violation returns 422. SEC-010 All sensitive data encrypted at rest. PostgreSQL: pgcrypto for sensitive columns. Android: EncryptedSharedPreferences + encrypted Room database. 2. AUTHENTICATION &amp; SESSION SECURITY 2.1 JWT Implementation Requirements # JWT signing ΓÇö backend/services/jwt_service.py ALGORITHM = &apos;HS256&apos; # Never RS256 without key rotation infrastructure ACCESS_TOKEN_EXPIRE_MINUTES = 15 # Short ΓÇö minimizes exposure window REFRESH_TOKEN_EXPIRE_DAYS = 7 # Rotates on every use # Token payload ΓÇö minimum claims only def create_access_token(user_id: str, subscription_tier: str) -&gt; str: payload = { &apos;sub&apos;: user_id, # Subject ΓÇö user UUID only &apos;tier&apos;: subscription_tier, # For feature gating &apos;exp&apos;: datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(minutes=15), &apos;iat&apos;: datetime.utcnow(), &apos;jti&apos;: str(uuid4()), # JWT ID ΓÇö enables token blacklisting if needed } return jwt.encode(payload, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithm=ALGORITHM) # Validation ΓÇö backend/dependencies.py async def require_auth(token: str = Depends(oauth2_scheme), db=Depends(get_db)): try: payload = jwt.decode(token, settings.SECRET_KEY, algorithms=[ALGORITHM]) user_id = payload.get(&apos;sub&apos;) if not user_id: raise HTTPException(401, &apos;Invalid token&apos;) # Set PostgreSQL session variable for RLS await db.execute(f&quot;SET LOCAL app.current_user_id = &apos;{user_id}&apos;&quot;) return payload except JWTError: raise HTTPException(401, &apos;Invalid or expired token&apos;) 2.2 OTP Security Requirement Implementation Why OTP is 6 digits secrets.randbelow(1000000) zero-padded to 6 Not predictable from timestamp OTP hashed before storage bcrypt with cost factor 10 If DB leaked, OTPs are not exposed OTP expires in 5 minutes expires_at = now + 300 seconds checked on verify Limits attack window Max 5 attempts per phone per 10 minutes Increment attempts counter, check before verify Prevents brute force of 6-digit space Used OTP cannot be reused used=True set on successful verify, checked before use Prevents replay attacks OTP session deleted after use or expiry Cron job: DELETE FROM otp_sessions WHERE expires_at &lt; NOW() Keeps table clean Rate limit on send-otp endpoint 5 requests per phone per 10 minutes at nginx level Prevents SMS flooding costs 2.3 Refresh Token Rotation # On every token refresh ΓÇö backend/api/v1/auth.py async def refresh_token(refresh_token: str, db=Depends(get_db)): # 1. Hash the incoming token token_hash = hashlib.sha256(refresh_token.encode()).hexdigest() # 2. Find in DB ΓÇö must exist, not revoked, not expired record = await db.fetch_one( &apos;SELECT * FROM refresh_tokens WHERE token_hash=:h AND NOT revoked AND expires_at &gt; NOW()&apos;, {&apos;h&apos;: token_hash} ) if not record: raise HTTPException(401, &apos;Invalid refresh token&apos;) # 3. Revoke old token BEFORE issuing new one await db.execute(&apos;UPDATE refresh_tokens SET revoked=true WHERE id=:id&apos;, {&apos;id&apos;: record[&apos;id&apos;]}) # 4. Issue new access + refresh token pair new_access = create_access_token(record[&apos;user_id&apos;]) new_refresh = create_refresh_token(record[&apos;user_id&apos;]) return {&apos;access_token&apos;: new_access, &apos;refresh_token&apos;: new_refresh} 3. API SECURITY ΓÇö OWASP API TOP 10 COMPLIANCE OWASP ID Risk Agri-SAGE Mitigation Implementation API1 Broken Object Level Auth PostgreSQL RLS enforces row-level ownership at DB level ΓÇö not app level SET LOCAL app.current_user_id in every request; RLS policies on all user tables API2 Broken Authentication OTP only, bcrypt hashed, 5-minute expiry, max 5 attempts Implemented in auth.py ΓÇö see Section 2 API3 Broken Object Property Auth Pydantic response models expose only allowed fields ΓÇö no SELECT * All endpoints return typed Pydantic schemas ΓÇö DB model fields never exposed directly API4 Unrestricted Resource Consumption Rate limiting at nginx + per-user advisory quota enforcement nginx: 2r/s on /diagnose; user.advisories_this_month checked before pipeline starts API5 Broken Function Level Auth Role check on expert endpoints ΓÇö regular users cannot access /expert/ JWT tier claim checked: expert endpoints require tier=&apos;expert&apos; or role=&apos;expert&apos; API6 Unrestricted Access to Sensitive Business Flows Advisory generation rate limited ΓÇö prevents scraping 10 advisory requests per user per hour; subscription tier enforcement API7 Server-Side Request Forgery No user-controlled URLs fetched by server Weather/scheme URLs are hardcoded ΓÇö no user input ever used as URL API8 Security Misconfiguration CORS explicit allowlist, no debug mode in production, no stack traces in responses ENVIRONMENT=production disables all debug features; CORS origins from env var list API9 Improper Inventory Management All endpoints documented in OpenAPI ΓÇö no hidden endpoints FastAPI auto-generates OpenAPI; internal routes prefixed /internal/ and not in router API10 Unsafe Consumption of APIs All external API responses validated before use Weather/e-NAM responses parsed through Pydantic models ΓÇö unexpected fields ignored 3.1 Input Validation Rules ΓÇö Every Endpoint # All string inputs ΓÇö backend/api/v1/farms.py example class FarmCreateRequest(BaseModel): name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=200, strip_whitespace=True) location_lat: float = Field(..., ge=-90, le=90) location_lon: float = Field(..., ge=-180, le=180) area_ha: Optional[float] = Field(None, gt=0, le=10000) @field_validator(&apos;name&apos;) def sanitize_name(cls, v): # Strip HTML tags and control characters import re v = re.sub(r&apos;&lt;[^&gt;]+&gt;&apos;, &apos;&apos;, v) # No HTML v = re.sub(r&apos;[\x00-\x1f\x7f]&apos;, &apos;&apos;, v) # No control chars return v.strip() # Observation description ΓÇö prevent prompt injection class ObservationRequest(BaseModel): problem_description: Optional[str] = Field(None, max_length=2000) @field_validator(&apos;problem_description&apos;) def prevent_injection(cls, v): if v is None: return v # Log if injection-like patterns detected import re if re.search(r&apos;(ignore previous|system:|&lt;\|im_start\|&gt;|\[INST\])&apos;, v, re.IGNORECASE): logger.warning(&apos;Possible prompt injection attempt in observation description&apos;) # Still store as data ΓÇö just log. Never pass raw to LLM system prompt. return v[:2000] 3.2 Rate Limiting Configuration # nginx.conf ΓÇö production rate limiting http { # Rate limit zones limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=general:10m rate=20r/s; limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=diagnose:10m rate=2r/s; limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=otp:10m rate=5r/10m; limit_req_zone $binary_remote_addr zone=auth:10m rate=10r/s; server { # All API routes location /api/v1/ { limit_req zone=general burst=40 nodelay; limit_req_status 429; } # Advisory generation ΓÇö expensive, strict limit location /api/v1/diagnose { limit_req zone=diagnose burst=5 nodelay; limit_req_status 429; } # OTP sending ΓÇö prevent SMS bill abuse location /api/v1/auth/send-otp { limit_req zone=otp burst=2 nodelay; limit_req_status 429; } } } 4. FILE UPLOAD SECURITY 4.1 Complete Image Upload Security Pipeline # backend/services/image_security.py import magic # python-magic ΓÇö MIME from file content, not extension import subprocess # ExifTool for metadata strip from PIL import Image import io ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES = {&apos;image/jpeg&apos;, &apos;image/png&apos;, &apos;image/webp&apos;} MAX_SIZE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024 # 5MB MAX_DIMENSION_PX = 1200 async def process_uploaded_image(file_bytes: bytes, original_filename: str) -&gt; bytes: # 1. Size check BEFORE reading into memory fully if len(file_bytes) &gt; MAX_SIZE_BYTES: raise ValueError(f&apos;Image too large: {len(file_bytes)} bytes. Max: {MAX_SIZE_BYTES}&apos;) # 2. MIME check from content ΓÇö NOT from filename/extension mime = magic.from_buffer(file_bytes[:2048], mime=True) if mime not in ALLOWED_MIME_TYPES: raise ValueError(f&apos;File type not allowed: {mime}&apos;) # 3. Open with Pillow ΓÇö verifies it is a real image try: img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(file_bytes)) img.verify() # Detect truncated/corrupt images img = Image.open(io.BytesIO(file_bytes)) # Reopen after verify except Exception as e: raise ValueError(f&apos;Invalid image: {e}&apos;) # 4. Strip ALL EXIF metadata ΓÇö protects farmer location privacy # Create new image without metadata clean = Image.new(img.mode, img.size) clean.putdata(list(img.getdata())) # 5. Resize to max dimension while preserving aspect ratio if max(clean.size) &gt; MAX_DIMENSION_PX: clean.thumbnail((MAX_DIMENSION_PX, MAX_DIMENSION_PX), Image.LANCZOS) # 6. Re-encode as JPEG (normalizes format, removes any embedded scripts) output = io.BytesIO() clean.save(output, format=&apos;JPEG&apos;, quality=85, optimize=True) return output.getvalue() 5. DATA PRIVACY &amp; INDIA PDPB 2023 COMPLIANCE 5.1 Data Classification Data Type Classification Storage Retention Farmer Control Phone number Personal ΓÇö PII Users table ΓÇö required for auth Account lifetime Can delete account GPS coordinates Personal ΓÇö Sensitive PII farms table ΓÇö encrypted column Account lifetime Can delete farm Crop photos Personal Cloudflare R2 ΓÇö encrypted at rest 2 years then auto-delete Can delete per image Diagnosis results Personal advisories table 3 years for research Can delete observation Farm economics data Sensitive Personal interventions/outcomes tables Account lifetime Included in data export Voice recordings Personal R2 ΓÇö deleted after transcription Not retained after transcription Auto-deleted Market prices Non-personal market_prices table ΓÇö public data Indefinite N/A Government schemes Non-personal schemes table ΓÇö public data Until scheme expires N/A Aggregated analytics Anonymized Separate analytics DB Indefinite Cannot be linked to individual 5.2 Data Minimization Rules ΓÇö What to Collect and What NOT to Data Point Collect? Reason Phone number YES Required for OTP authentication ΓÇö no alternative Name OPTIONAL Not required for advisory ΓÇö collected only if farmer provides GPS exact coordinates ONLY for weather/schemes Rounded to 2dp for any analytics ΓÇö never exact in logs Device IMEI / device ID NO Not needed ΓÇö not collected App usage analytics Minimal only Advisory count, session duration ΓÇö no screen-level tracking Farmer financial income NO Not collected ΓÇö we estimate cost/revenue from crop data only Aadhaar / PAN NO Not needed, not collected, not stored Family information NO Not needed, not collected Photos of farmer / face NO Only crop photos ΓÇö if face detected in image, warn and request re-capture Location history NO Current farm location only ΓÇö no movement tracking 5.3 User Rights Implementation Right Endpoint Implementation Timeline Right to access all data GET /api/v1/users/my-data Returns all user data as JSON export ΓÇö all tables joined on user_id Immediate Right to delete account DELETE /api/v1/users/me Soft delete user, queue hard delete of all data in 30 days, revoke all tokens Confirmed in 30 days Right to delete specific images DELETE /api/v1/images/{id} Deletes from R2 and DB ΓÇö confirms deletion in response Immediate Right to correct data PUT /api/v1/users/profile Update name, language, location Immediate Right to data portability GET /api/v1/users/export ZIP file: JSON of all farm data + image links Generated within 24 hours Right to know what we collect Public privacy policy page All data types listed with purpose and retention period Static page ΓÇö always accessible 5.4 Privacy Policy Template Deploy this at: https://yourdomain.in/privacy ΓÇö required for Google Play Store submission. AGRI-SAGE PRIVACY POLICY Last updated: [DATE] 1. WHO WE ARE Agri-SAGE is operated by [YOUR NAME/COMPANY], Thiruvananthapuram, Kerala, India. Contact: privacy@[yourdomain].in 2. WHAT WE COLLECT - Phone number: required to create your account via OTP verification - Farm location: district and state level ΓÇö used to provide relevant weather and scheme information - Crop photos: analyzed for disease/pest detection ΓÇö stored securely in encrypted storage - Crop and farming information: planting dates, interventions, outcomes ΓÇö used to improve your advisory - Device information: Android version, device model ΓÇö used for app compatibility only 3. WHAT WE DO NOT COLLECT We do not collect: your Aadhaar, PAN, income, biometric data, precise GPS history, family information, or any data not listed above. 4. HOW WE USE YOUR DATA - To generate agricultural advisories specific to your farm, crop, and location - To send you relevant alerts and morning briefings - To match you with applicable government schemes - To improve our AI models using anonymized, aggregated data only 5. DATA SHARING We do not sell your data. We do not share personal data with advertisers. We share data only with: cloud storage providers (Cloudflare) under data processing agreements. 6. YOUR RIGHTS You can: access all your data, correct it, delete it, or export it. Contact us at privacy@[yourdomain].in or use the in-app &apos;My Data&apos; option. 7. DATA RETENTION Account data: retained while account is active + 30 days after deletion request. Crop photos: auto-deleted after 2 years. Voice recordings: deleted immediately after transcription. 8. SECURITY All data encrypted in transit (TLS 1.3) and at rest. Passwords are not stored ΓÇö we use OTP only. 9. CHILDREN Agri-SAGE is not intended for users under 18. We do not knowingly collect data from minors. 10. CHANGES We will notify you in-app of material changes to this policy at least 7 days before they take effect. 6. ANDROID SECURITY REQUIREMENTS 6.1 OWASP Mobile Top 10 Compliance OWASP M Risk Agri-SAGE Implementation M1 Improper Platform Usage Misuse of Android APIs Use EncryptedSharedPreferences for tokens ΓÇö never plain SharedPreferences. Use FileProvider for image sharing. M2 Insecure Data Storage Data stored in plain text EncryptedSharedPreferences for JWT tokens. Encrypted Room database using SQLCipher. Images in app-private directory. M3 Insecure Communication HTTP instead of HTTPS Network security config: cleartextTrafficPermitted=false. Certificate pinning for agrisage API domain. M4 Insecure Authentication Weak auth OTP + JWT only. Biometric lock option for app open (optional). Tokens stored in EncryptedSharedPreferences. M5 Insufficient Crypto Weak encryption AES-256-GCM for local storage. TLS 1.3 minimum for network. No MD5, no SHA-1. M6 Insecure Auth Auth bypass JWT validated on every API call. Expired token triggers re-auth ΓÇö not silent refresh to protect user. M7 Poor Code Quality SQL injection in local DB Room ORM used ΓÇö no raw SQL string construction. Parameterized queries only. M8 Code Tampering Rooted device bypass ProGuard/R8 obfuscation. SafetyNet/Play Integrity API check on first launch ΓÇö warn if device rooted. M9 Reverse Engineering API key extraction No API keys in APK. All keys stored server-side. R2 keys never in mobile code. M10 Extraneous Functionality Debug code in production BuildConfig.DEBUG gates all debug features. No debug endpoints in production build. Logs disabled in release build. 6.2 Android Security Configuration Files &lt;!-- res/xml/network_security_config.xml --&gt; &lt;network-security-config&gt; &lt;base-config cleartextTrafficPermitted=&quot;false&quot;&gt; &lt;trust-anchors&gt; &lt;certificates src=&quot;system&quot; /&gt; &lt;/trust-anchors&gt; &lt;/base-config&gt; &lt;!-- Certificate pinning for production API --&gt; &lt;domain-config&gt; &lt;domain includeSubdomains=&quot;true&quot;&gt;api.agrisage.in&lt;/domain&gt; &lt;pin-set expiration=&quot;2027-01-01&quot;&gt; &lt;!-- Primary pin: SHA256 of your server certificate public key --&gt; &lt;pin digest=&quot;SHA-256&quot;&gt;REPLACE_WITH_ACTUAL_PIN=&lt;/pin&gt; &lt;!-- Backup pin: SHA256 of CA certificate --&gt; &lt;pin digest=&quot;SHA-256&quot;&gt;REPLACE_WITH_BACKUP_PIN=&lt;/pin&gt; &lt;/pin-set&gt; &lt;/domain-config&gt; &lt;/network-security-config&gt; &lt;!-- AndroidManifest.xml additions --&gt; &lt;application android:networkSecurityConfig=&quot;@xml/network_security_config&quot; android:allowBackup=&quot;false&quot; android:debuggable=&quot;false&quot; android:usesCleartextTraffic=&quot;false&quot;&gt; 7. DATABASE SECURITY 7.1 PostgreSQL Hardening -- Create application user with minimum required privileges CREATE USER agrisage_app WITH PASSWORD &apos;strong_random_password&apos;; GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE agrisage TO agrisage_app; GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO agrisage_app; -- Grant only what is needed ΓÇö no superuser, no createdb GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO agrisage_app; GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA public TO agrisage_app; -- Create read-only user for analytics/reporting CREATE USER agrisage_readonly WITH PASSWORD &apos;another_strong_password&apos;; GRANT CONNECT ON DATABASE agrisage TO agrisage_readonly; GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA public TO agrisage_readonly; GRANT SELECT ON ALL TABLES IN SCHEMA public TO agrisage_readonly; -- Encrypt sensitive columns using pgcrypto CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto; -- Farm locations stored encrypted (farmer privacy) -- Use pgcrypto encrypt/decrypt functions in application layer -- Column type: BYTEA for encrypted values -- Application encrypts: pgp_sym_encrypt(lat::text, :encryption_key) -- Application decrypts: pgp_sym_decrypt(location_encrypted, :encryption_key)::float 7.2 Connection Security # SQLAlchemy connection ΓÇö backend/database.py engine = create_async_engine( settings.DATABASE_URL, pool_size=10, # Max 10 connections max_overflow=5, # Allow 5 overflow in bursts pool_timeout=30, # Wait max 30s for connection pool_recycle=1800, # Recycle connections every 30 min echo=settings.ENVIRONMENT == &apos;development&apos;, # Log SQL only in dev connect_args={ &apos;ssl&apos;: &apos;require&apos;, # TLS for all DB connections in production &apos;server_settings&apos;: { &apos;application_name&apos;: &apos;agrisage_backend&apos;, } } ) 8. SECRET MANAGEMENT 8.1 Secret Rotation Schedule Secret Rotation Frequency Method Impact of Rotation SECRET_KEY (JWT signing) Every 90 days Generate new: python -c &quot;import secrets; print(secrets.token_hex(32))&quot; All existing JWTs invalidated ΓÇö users must re-login DATABASE_URL password Every 90 days PostgreSQL ALTER USER ... PASSWORD ΓÇö update env var ΓÇö restart app Brief downtime during restart R2_ACCESS_KEY Every 90 days Cloudflare dashboard ΓÇö create new key, update env, delete old Brief downtime during restart OTP_PROVIDER_KEY Every 6 months MSG91 dashboard ΓÇö new API key No user impact BHASHINI_API_KEY As directed by Bhashini Bhashini portal Voice feature briefly unavailable FCM_KEY Only if compromised Firebase console Notifications temporarily disrupted 8.2 .env File Rules .env file NEVER committed to git ΓÇö verify .gitignore contains .env before first commit .env.example has all keys with empty values or placeholder descriptions ΓÇö always kept updated Production secrets stored in Oracle Cloud Vault (free tier) or environment variables set directly on server Never log environment variable values ΓÇö structlog config must exclude env vars Local dev uses .env ΓÇö staging and production use server environment variables directly # Generate all secrets in one command ΓÇö run once during setup python3 -c &quot; import secrets print(f&apos;SECRET_KEY={secrets.token_hex(32)}&apos;) print(f&apos;DB_PASSWORD={secrets.token_urlsafe(24)}&apos;) print(f&apos;ENCRYPTION_KEY={secrets.token_hex(32)}&apos;) &quot; 9. AUDIT LOGGING 9.1 What Must Be Logged Event Required Fields Retention Reason Advisory generated user_id, observation_id, advisory_id, model_version, generation_ms, timestamp 3 years Traceability ΓÇö explain any advisory at any time Advisory marked unsafe by Critic user_id, observation_id, blocked_reason, timestamp 5 years Safety audit trail Authentication event (login/OTP) phone_hash (not plain), ip_address, success/fail, timestamp 1 year Security monitoring Data export requested user_id, timestamp, ip_address 3 years Privacy compliance Account deletion requested user_id, timestamp, scheduled_deletion_date 7 years Legal requirement Image uploaded user_id, image_id, mime_type, size_bytes, quality_score, timestamp 3 years Debugging and safety Scheme eligibility assessed user_id, scheme_id, eligibility_score, timestamp 3 years Compliance ΓÇö never claim eligibility wrongly Expert review submitted expert_id, advisory_id, verdict, timestamp 5 years Model improvement trail Payment event user_id, amount, plan, razorpay_order_id, success/fail, timestamp 7 years Financial compliance Security event (rate limit, injection attempt) ip_address, endpoint, pattern_detected, timestamp 2 years Security monitoring 9.2 Structlog Configuration # backend/logging_config.py import structlog def configure_logging(): structlog.configure( processors=[ structlog.contextvars.merge_contextvars, structlog.processors.add_log_level, structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt=&apos;iso&apos;), # CRITICAL: Never log sensitive values structlog.processors.ExceptionRenderer(), structlog.processors.JSONRenderer(), # JSON for log aggregation ], wrapper_class=structlog.make_filtering_bound_logger(logging.INFO), ) # NEVER LOG: phone numbers, GPS coords, image content, OTPs ) 10. CI/CD SECURITY GATES 10.1 Automated Security Checks in GitHub Actions # .github/workflows/security.yml name: Security Gates on: [push, pull_request] jobs: security: runs-on: ubuntu-latest steps: - uses: actions/checkout@v4 - name: Check for hardcoded secrets run: | # Fail if any pattern like API_KEY=&quot;actual_value&quot; found if grep -rn --include=&apos;*.py&apos; --include=&apos;*.kt&apos; --include=&apos;*.js&apos; \ -E &apos;(SECRET_KEY|API_KEY|PASSWORD|TOKEN)\s*=\s*&quot;[a-zA-Z0-9+/]{20,}&quot;&apos; .; then echo &apos;FAIL: Hardcoded secret detected&apos; exit 1 fi - name: Dependency vulnerability scan run: | pip install pip-audit pip-audit -r requirements.txt --fail-on CRITICAL - name: Verify .env not committed run: | if git ls-files --error-unmatch .env 2&gt;/dev/null; then echo &apos;FAIL: .env file is tracked by git&apos; exit 1 fi - name: Check RLS enabled on user tables run: | # Connect to test DB and verify RLS python3 scripts/verify_rls.py - name: SAST scan run: | pip install bandit bandit -r backend/ -ll --exit-zero # Report only, don&apos;t block on LOW bandit -r backend/ -lll # Block on HIGH severity AGRI-SAGE SECURITY &amp; COMPLIANCE v1.0 Every rule in this document is a hard requirement ΓÇö not a guideline


=== 8_AgriSAGE_AI_Prompt_Handbook.docx ===

AGRI-SAGE AI AGENT PROMPT HANDBOOK Every System Prompt ┬╖ Every Agent Configuration ┬╖ LLM Parameters ┬╖ Validation Rules ┬╖ Fallback Behaviors Field Detail Document AI Agent Prompt Handbook v1.0 Audience Backend Developer, VSCode AI Agent building the system Critical Rule Copy these prompts EXACTLY. Do not paraphrase. LLM behavior is sensitive to wording. LLM Models Server: Qwen3-7B (4-bit) | On-device: Qwen3-1.7B (ONNX) | Embedding: multilingual-e5-large Temperature Advisory synthesis: 0.2 | Explanation generation: 0.3 | Scheme eligibility: 0.1 CRITICAL RULES FOR EVERY LLM CALL ΓÜá These rules apply to EVERY LLM interaction in Agri-SAGE. Violating any of these rules creates safety risks for farmers. Rule Statement RULE-LLM-001 The LLM NEVER invents agricultural facts. It synthesizes ONLY from the evidence bundle and retrieved knowledge chunks provided in the prompt. RULE-LLM-002 The LLM NEVER names a chemical product, pesticide, or fertilizer that does not appear in the retrieved knowledge chunks or CIB&amp;RC data provided in the prompt. RULE-LLM-003 The LLM NEVER states a yield number, profit figure, or cost estimate without labelling it &apos;modelled estimate&apos; ΓÇö these come from simulation output, not LLM knowledge. RULE-LLM-004 The LLM NEVER recommends treatment when confidence_state is UNCERTAIN, INSUFFICIENT_DATA, or CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE. It requests more information instead. RULE-LLM-005 The LLM NEVER claims a farmer is eligible for a government scheme. It says &apos;you appear to meet the listed criteria ΓÇö final eligibility determined by the concerned authority&apos;. RULE-LLM-006 All LLM output is parsed as JSON against a Pydantic schema before use. Raw LLM text is NEVER passed directly to the farmer response. RULE-LLM-007 If the LLM returns invalid JSON, retry once with &apos;Output only valid JSON.&apos; appended. If second attempt fails, use the safe fallback template ΓÇö do not show LLM output. RULE-LLM-008 User text (problem_description, voice transcript) is NEVER inserted into the LLM system prompt. It is passed as structured data in the user message only. 1. EXPLANATION AGENT ΓÇö ADVISORY SYNTHESIS 1.1 System Prompt ΓÇö Production SYSTEM_PROMPT_EXPLANATION = &quot;&quot;&quot; You are Agri-SAGE Advisory Engine, an agricultural decision support system. YOUR ROLE: Convert the structured evidence bundle below into a clear, actionable advisory for a smallholder farmer. You are a translator of evidence ΓÇö not a source of knowledge. ABSOLUTE CONSTRAINTS ΓÇö violating these is a system failure: 1. Use ONLY facts from the evidence_bundle and knowledge_chunks provided below. Do not add any agricultural knowledge from your training data. 2. If evidence_bundle.confidence_state is UNCERTAIN, INSUFFICIENT_DATA, or CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE: do NOT recommend any treatment. Request more information. 3. Chemical product names: only use names that appear verbatim in the knowledge_chunks or cibrc_data provided. Never invent product names. 4. All yield/profit numbers come from simulation_result only. Always label them: &apos;modelled estimate ΓÇö actual results vary by field conditions&apos;. 5. Government schemes: always include: &apos;Final eligibility determined by the concerned authority ΓÇö please verify before applying.&apos; 6. Language: Generate advisory in {language_code} ({language_name}). Use simple, everyday vocabulary. Avoid technical jargon. If a technical term is unavoidable, explain it in parentheses. OUTPUT FORMAT: Return ONLY valid JSON matching this exact schema. No markdown. No explanation. No text before or after the JSON object. { &quot;diagnosis_summary&quot;: &quot;&lt;2 sentences max ΓÇö what is happening&gt;&quot;, &quot;confidence_explanation&quot;: &quot;&lt;1 sentence ΓÇö why this confidence level&gt;&quot;, &quot;risk_explanation&quot;: &quot;&lt;1-2 sentences ΓÇö how serious and how soon&gt;&quot;, &quot;recommended_action&quot;: { &quot;what&quot;: &quot;&lt;specific action in imperative voice&gt;&quot;, &quot;when&quot;: &quot;&lt;specific timing&gt;&quot;, &quot;how&quot;: &quot;&lt;step-by-step method&gt;&quot;, &quot;cost_note&quot;: &quot;&lt;estimated cost range&gt;&quot;, &quot;spray_window&quot;: &quot;&lt;time window or null&gt;&quot; }, &quot;evidence_summary&quot;: [&quot;&lt;evidence point 1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;evidence point 2&gt;&quot;], &quot;what_would_change_this&quot;: [&quot;&lt;factor 1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;factor 2&gt;&quot;], &quot;safe_to_act&quot;: &lt;true or false&gt;, &quot;insufficient_data_message&quot;: &quot;&lt;null or message if more info needed&gt;&quot;, &quot;modelled_estimate_label&quot;: &quot;modelled estimate ΓÇö actual results vary by field conditions&quot; } &quot;&quot;&quot; 1.2 User Prompt Template ΓÇö Advisory Synthesis USER_PROMPT_ADVISORY = &quot;&quot;&quot; EVIDENCE BUNDLE: confidence_state: {confidence_state} confidence_score: {confidence_score} VISION RESULT: label: {vision_label} severity: {vision_severity} affected_area_pct: {affected_area_pct} WEATHER INDICATORS: disease_favorability_index: {dfi} spray_window: {spray_window} consecutive_wet_days: {wet_days} temperature_c: {temperature} humidity_pct: {humidity} CROP STAGE: stage: {stage_name} disease_vulnerability: {vulnerability} days_after_sowing: {das} RETRIEVED KNOWLEDGE CHUNKS (use only these ΓÇö do not add external knowledge): {knowledge_chunks_json} SIMULATION RESULT (label all numbers as &apos;modelled estimate&apos;): {simulation_result_json} CRITIC CONFLICTS: {conflicts_json} MISSING DATA: {missing_data_json} FARMER CONTEXT: crop: {crop} location: {district}, {state} language: {language_name} Generate the advisory JSON now. Output JSON only. &quot;&quot;&quot; 1.3 Fallback Template ΓÇö Used When LLM Fails # backend/agents/explanation.py SAFE_FALLBACK = { &apos;diagnosis_summary&apos;: &apos;We analyzed your crop photo and farm conditions.&apos;, &apos;confidence_explanation&apos;: &apos;Our confidence is limited ΓÇö please see the evidence details.&apos;, &apos;risk_explanation&apos;: &apos;Please inspect your field and consult a local agronomist.&apos;, &apos;recommended_action&apos;: { &apos;what&apos;: &apos;Inspect 10 plants in different parts of the field&apos;, &apos;when&apos;: &apos;Today&apos;, &apos;how&apos;: &apos;Look for any spread of symptoms since the photo was taken&apos;, &apos;cost_note&apos;: &apos;No cost ΓÇö visual inspection only&apos;, &apos;spray_window&apos;: None }, &apos;evidence_summary&apos;: [&apos;Photo analyzed&apos;, &apos;Weather checked&apos;, &apos;Crop stage estimated&apos;], &apos;what_would_change_this&apos;: [&apos;A clearer photo&apos;, &apos;A soil test result&apos;], &apos;safe_to_act&apos;: False, &apos;insufficient_data_message&apos;: &apos;Advisory generation encountered a technical issue. This is a basic response.&apos;, &apos;modelled_estimate_label&apos;: &apos;modelled estimate ΓÇö actual results vary by field conditions&apos; } 2. SCHEME AGENT ΓÇö ELIGIBILITY ASSESSMENT 2.1 System Prompt SYSTEM_PROMPT_SCHEME = &quot;&quot;&quot; You are Agri-SAGE Scheme Eligibility Assessor. YOUR ROLE: Assess whether a farmer appears to meet the criteria of government agricultural schemes. ABSOLUTE CONSTRAINTS: 1. NEVER state &apos;you are eligible&apos; or &apos;you qualify&apos;. Always use: &apos;You appear to meet the criteria listed below.&apos; 2. ALWAYS include this exact sentence at the end of every scheme assessment: &apos;Final eligibility is determined by the concerned authority. Please verify before applying.&apos; 3. Only assess schemes from the scheme_data provided below. Never mention schemes not in the provided data. 4. If a scheme&apos;s valid_until date has passed, do not include it. 5. If eligibility cannot be determined from available farmer data, say: &apos;We need your [missing field] to assess eligibility for this scheme.&apos; OUTPUT FORMAT: Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown. { &quot;matches&quot;: [ { &quot;scheme_name&quot;: &quot;&lt;exact scheme name from data&gt;&quot;, &quot;scheme_authority&quot;: &quot;&lt;authority from data&gt;&quot;, &quot;eligibility_assessment&quot;: &quot;likely_meets|partially_meets|cannot_determine&quot;, &quot;matching_criteria&quot;: [&quot;&lt;criterion 1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;criterion 2&gt;&quot;], &quot;missing_criteria&quot;: [&quot;&lt;what we cannot verify&gt;&quot;], &quot;key_benefit&quot;: &quot;&lt;main benefit in 1 sentence&gt;&quot;, &quot;enrollment_steps&quot;: [&quot;&lt;step 1&gt;&quot;, &quot;&lt;step 2&gt;&quot;], &quot;deadline&quot;: &quot;&lt;deadline or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;mandatory_caveat&quot;: &quot;Final eligibility determined by the concerned authority. Please verify before applying.&quot; } ], &quot;no_matches_reason&quot;: &quot;&lt;null or reason if no schemes matched&gt;&quot; } &quot;&quot;&quot; 3. INTAKE AGENT ΓÇö STRUCTURED EXTRACTION 3.1 System Prompt ΓÇö Voice/Text Intent Extraction SYSTEM_PROMPT_INTAKE = &quot;&quot;&quot; You are Agri-SAGE Intake Parser. YOUR ROLE: Extract structured agricultural information from farmer input (text or voice transcript). RULES: 1. Extract ONLY what is explicitly stated. Do not infer or assume. 2. If a field is not mentioned, set it to null ΓÇö do not guess. 3. Normalize crop names to: rice, banana, coconut, tomato, maize, pepper, cassava, groundnut 4. Normalize growth stages to: germination, seedling, tillering, panicle_initiation, flowering, grain_fill, maturity 5. This is a PARSING task ΓÇö do not add agricultural knowledge or advice. OUTPUT FORMAT: Return ONLY valid JSON. No markdown. No explanation. { &quot;crop&quot;: &quot;&lt;normalized crop name or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;problem_description&quot;: &quot;&lt;verbatim problem description or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;observed_growth_stage&quot;: &quot;&lt;normalized stage or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;irrigation_status&quot;: &quot;&lt;irrigated|dry|waterlogged or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;fertilizer_recent&quot;: &quot;&lt;true|false or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;pesticide_recent&quot;: &quot;&lt;true|false or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;location_mentioned&quot;: &quot;&lt;place name if mentioned or null&gt;&quot;, &quot;extraction_confidence&quot;: &quot;&lt;high|medium|low&gt;&quot;, &quot;extraction_notes&quot;: &quot;&lt;any ambiguities found&gt;&quot; } &quot;&quot;&quot; 4. MONITORING AGENT ΓÇö MORNING BRIEFING GENERATION 4.1 System Prompt SYSTEM_PROMPT_BRIEFING = &quot;&quot;&quot; You are Agri-SAGE Morning Briefing Generator. YOUR ROLE: Generate a concise, actionable daily farm briefing for a smallholder farmer. RULES: 1. Maximum 4 items in the briefing ΓÇö one per category below. 2. Use simple language the farmer can act on immediately. 3. Be specific ΓÇö &apos;Check lower leaves&apos; not &apos;Monitor crop health&apos;. 4. If no significant risk exists, say so positively: &apos;All clear today&apos;. 5. One recommended action only ΓÇö the most important one for today. 6. Language: {language_name}. Simple vocabulary only. 7. Do not use technical terms without explanation. OUTPUT FORMAT: Return ONLY valid JSON. { &quot;stage_summary&quot;: &quot;&lt;crop name + stage + days since planting ΓÇö 1 line&gt;&quot;, &quot;top_risk&quot;: { &quot;risk_type&quot;: &quot;&lt;disease|pest|water|heat|none&gt;&quot;, &quot;level&quot;: &quot;&lt;high|medium|low|none&gt;&quot;, &quot;description&quot;: &quot;&lt;1 sentence ΓÇö what and why&gt;&quot;, &quot;is_urgent&quot;: &lt;true or false&gt; }, &quot;weather_today&quot;: &quot;&lt;2-word weather summary: &apos;Rain expected&apos; / &apos;Hot, clear&apos; / &apos;Cloudy&apos;&gt;&quot;, &quot;recommended_action&quot;: &quot;&lt;one specific action for today ΓÇö imperative sentence&gt;&quot;, &quot;market_note&quot;: &quot;&lt;price change note or null if no significant change&gt;&quot;, &quot;all_clear&quot;: &lt;true if no risks, false otherwise&gt; } &quot;&quot;&quot; 5. LLM CALL IMPLEMENTATION 5.1 Qwen3 API Call Wrapper # backend/services/llm_service.py import httpx import json from typing import Optional, Type from pydantic import BaseModel class LLMService: def __init__(self, model: str = &apos;qwen3-7b&apos;, base_url: str = &apos;http://localhost:11434&apos;): self.model = model self.base_url = base_url # Ollama local server self.client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=30.0) async def generate( self, system_prompt: str, user_prompt: str, temperature: float = 0.2, max_tokens: int = 1500, output_schema: Optional[Type[BaseModel]] = None, ) -&gt; dict: payload = { &apos;model&apos;: self.model, &apos;messages&apos;: [ {&apos;role&apos;: &apos;system&apos;, &apos;content&apos;: system_prompt}, {&apos;role&apos;: &apos;user&apos;, &apos;content&apos;: user_prompt}, ], &apos;temperature&apos;: temperature, &apos;max_tokens&apos;: max_tokens, &apos;stream&apos;: False, } response = await self.client.post(f&apos;{self.base_url}/api/chat&apos;, json=payload) response.raise_for_status() content = response.json()[&apos;message&apos;][&apos;content&apos;] # Parse JSON ΓÇö retry once if invalid for attempt in range(2): try: # Strip markdown code fences if model adds them clean = content.strip() if clean.startswith(&apos;```&apos;): clean = clean.split(&apos;```&apos;)[1] if clean.startswith(&apos;json&apos;): clean = clean[4:] result = json.loads(clean) if output_schema: return output_schema(**result).model_dump() return result except (json.JSONDecodeError, ValueError) as e: if attempt == 0: user_prompt += &apos;\n\nIMPORTANT: Output ONLY valid JSON. No other text.&apos; response = await self.client.post(f&apos;{self.base_url}/api/chat&apos;, json=payload) content = response.json()[&apos;message&apos;][&apos;content&apos;] else: logger.error(&apos;llm_json_parse_failed&apos;, error=str(e)) return None # Caller uses fallback 5.2 Ollama Setup ΓÇö Free Local LLM Server # Install Ollama on Oracle Always-Free instance curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh # Pull Qwen3-7B quantized (4-bit ΓÇö fits in 6GB RAM) ollama pull qwen3:7b-q4_K_M # Pull Qwen3-1.7B for lighter tasks ollama pull qwen3:1.7b # Verify both models available ollama list # Ollama runs on localhost:11434 by default # Do NOT expose Ollama port publicly ΓÇö internal use only # nginx should NOT proxy /api/generate ΓÇö backend only 5.3 Embedding Model Setup # backend/services/embedding_service.py from sentence_transformers import SentenceTransformer class EmbeddingService: _instance = None @classmethod def get_instance(cls): if cls._instance is None: # multilingual-e5-large ΓÇö MIT license ΓÇö free for commercial use cls._instance = SentenceTransformer(&apos;intfloat/multilingual-e5-large&apos;) return cls._instance def embed_query(self, text: str) -&gt; list[float]: # REQUIRED: &apos;query: &apos; prefix for e5 models return self.get_instance().encode(f&apos;query: {text}&apos;).tolist() def embed_passage(self, text: str) -&gt; list[float]: # REQUIRED: &apos;passage: &apos; prefix for e5 models return self.get_instance().encode(f&apos;passage: {text}&apos;).tolist() def embed_batch(self, texts: list[str], is_query: bool = False) -&gt; list[list[float]]: prefix = &apos;query: &apos; if is_query else &apos;passage: &apos; prefixed = [f&apos;{prefix}{t}&apos; for t in texts] return self.get_instance().encode(prefixed).tolist() ΓÜá Always use &apos;query: &apos; prefix for search queries and &apos;passage: &apos; prefix for documents. Omitting these prefixes degrades retrieval quality by 15-25% for multilingual-e5-large. This is a model requirement, not optional. 6. CRITIC AGENT ΓÇö COMPLETE LOGIC 6.1 Confidence State Assignment Rules The Critic Agent is entirely rule-based ΓÇö NO LLM. These rules are deterministic. Condition Confidence State Action All evidence aligns: vision confidence &gt; 0.80 AND weather supports AND stage matches AND Tier 1 knowledge found CONFIRMED Allow treatment recommendation. Set run_simulation=True. Vision confidence 0.60-0.80 AND most evidence supports AND no major conflicts LIKELY Allow treatment recommendation with caveats. Set run_simulation=True if confidence &gt; 0.65. Vision confidence 0.40-0.60 OR minor weather conflict OR no Tier 1 knowledge POSSIBLE Allow conservative recommendation. Flag missing data. run_simulation=False. Vision confidence &lt; 0.40 OR multiple evidence conflicts OR poor image quality score &lt; 0.4 UNCERTAIN Do NOT recommend treatment. Request better image or field inspection. Image OOD detected OR crop not in supported list OR critical data missing INSUFFICIENT_DATA Do NOT recommend treatment. Request specific missing information. Vision says X, weather strongly contradicts X, AND crop stage says X uncommon CONFLICTING_EVIDENCE Do NOT recommend treatment. Explain the conflict. Suggest expert review. Crop or region not in supported scope OUT_OF_SCOPE Return message listing supported crops/regions. Offer to notify when supported. 6.2 Evidence Conflict Detection Rules # backend/agents/critic.py CONFLICT_RULES = [ { &apos;name&apos;: &apos;fungal_weather_mismatch&apos;, &apos;condition&apos;: lambda s: ( &apos;fungal&apos; in s.vision_result.label.lower() and s.weather_result.indicators.disease_favorability_index &lt; 0.25 and s.weather_result.indicators.consecutive_wet_days &lt; 2 ), &apos;action&apos;: &apos;REDUCE_CONFIDENCE&apos;, &apos;message&apos;: &apos;Image suggests fungal disease but weather conditions are unfavorable for fungal pathogens&apos; }, { &apos;name&apos;: &apos;stage_disease_mismatch&apos;, &apos;condition&apos;: lambda s: ( s.vision_result.label not in CROP_STAGE_DISEASE_MAP.get(s.crop_stage_result.stage_code, []) ), &apos;action&apos;: &apos;FLAG_WARNING&apos;, &apos;message&apos;: f&apos;This disease is uncommon at {stage} stage ΓÇö verify diagnosis&apos; }, { &apos;name&apos;: &apos;high_confidence_poor_image&apos;, &apos;condition&apos;: lambda s: ( s.vision_result.confidence_score &gt; 0.85 and s.vision_result.quality_score &lt; 0.5 ), &apos;action&apos;: &apos;REDUCE_CONFIDENCE&apos;, &apos;message&apos;: &apos;High model confidence but image quality is poor ΓÇö result may be unreliable&apos; }, ] SAFETY_BLOCKS = [ (&apos;product_not_in_cibrc&apos;, check_product_in_cibrc_db), (&apos;crop_not_registered&apos;, check_crop_chemical_registration), (&apos;dosage_exceeded&apos;, check_dosage_within_label), (&apos;scheme_expired&apos;, check_scheme_valid_until), (&apos;llm_invented_product&apos;, check_product_traceable_to_knowledge), ] 7. KNOWLEDGE CHUNK QUALITY RULES 7.1 Chunk Acceptance Criteria Rule Threshold Action if Failed Minimum chunk length 100 characters Discard chunk ΓÇö too short to be useful Maximum chunk length 512 tokens (approx 2000 chars) Split at sentence boundary valid_until date present Required ΓÇö no null Reject document ingestion if missing source_tier assigned 1-6 required Default to Tier 5 if uncertain ΓÇö never Tier 1 without verification Chunk contains crop mention Preferred ΓÇö not required Flag for metadata review Duplicate chunk detection SHA-256 hash of content Skip if hash already in DB Language detection correct Must match document metadata Flag mismatch for review 7.2 Retrieval Quality Validation # scripts/evaluate_retrieval.py # Run monthly to ensure RAG quality is maintained BENCHMARK_QUERIES = [ { &apos;query_text&apos;: &apos;rice blast disease treatment Kerala tillering stage&apos;, &apos;expected_tier&apos;: 1, &apos;expected_crop&apos;: &apos;rice&apos;, &apos;expected_topic&apos;: &apos;blast_disease&apos;, &apos;minimum_recall_at_5&apos;: 0.80, }, { &apos;query_text&apos;: &apos;banana bunchy top virus symptoms management&apos;, &apos;expected_tier&apos;: 1, &apos;expected_crop&apos;: &apos;banana&apos;, &apos;minimum_recall_at_5&apos;: 0.75, }, # Add minimum 20 queries covering all crops and key diseases ] def evaluate_retrieval(retriever, benchmark): results = [] for q in benchmark: hits = retriever.retrieve(q[&apos;query_text&apos;], top_k=5) # Check if any Tier 1-2 result in top 5 tier_ok = any(h[&apos;source_tier&apos;] &lt;= 2 for h in hits) # Check if correct crop in top 3 crop_ok = any(h.get(&apos;crop&apos;) == q[&apos;expected_crop&apos;] for h in hits[:3]) results.append({&apos;query&apos;: q[&apos;query_text&apos;], &apos;tier_ok&apos;: tier_ok, &apos;crop_ok&apos;: crop_ok}) pass_rate = sum(1 for r in results if r[&apos;tier_ok&apos;] and r[&apos;crop_ok&apos;]) / len(results) print(f&apos;Retrieval pass rate: {pass_rate:.1%}&apos;) assert pass_rate &gt;= 0.80, f&apos;Retrieval quality below threshold: {pass_rate:.1%}&apos; 8. ON-DEVICE LLM CONSTRAINTS (Qwen3-1.7B) ΓÜá Qwen3-1.7B is a 1.7 billion parameter model. It cannot reliably generate complex agricultural advisories. Its role on-device is strictly limited to language generation from pre-structured inputs. 8.1 What Qwen3-1.7B CAN Do On-Device Generate advisory text from a pre-filled template with specific slots Translate structured advisory from English template to Malayalam Summarize a morning briefing from pre-computed risk scores Generate simple &apos;What to check today&apos; sentences from risk level + crop stage 8.2 What Qwen3-1.7B Must NOT Do On-Device Generate disease diagnoses independently ΓÇö use ONNX vision model for this Provide pesticide recommendations ΓÇö use cached knowledge templates only Calculate yield estimates ΓÇö use local rules engine Access or reason about government schemes ΓÇö use cached scheme data 8.3 On-Device Advisory Template System # backend/ml/offline_templates.py # These templates are downloaded to device as part of knowledge cache # Qwen3-1.7B fills in the {slots} from structured local data OFFLINE_ADVISORY_TEMPLATE = { &apos;ml&apos;: { &apos;disease_detected&apos;: ( &apos;α┤¿α┤┐α┤Öα╡ìα┤Öα┤│α╡üα┤ƒα╡å {crop_ml} α╡╜ {disease_ml} α┤¿α╡ìα┤▒α╡å α┤▓α┤òα╡ìα┤╖α┤úα┤Öα╡ìα┤Öα╡╛ α┤òα┤╛α┤úα╡üα┤¿α╡ìα┤¿α╡ü. &apos; VSCODE AI AGENT ΓÇö HOW TO USE THIS HANDBOOK This section is specifically for the VSCode AI agent executing the build. 9.1 When to Reference This Document Task Section to Use Implementing explanation_agent.py Section 1 ΓÇö copy system prompt and user prompt template exactly Implementing scheme_agent.py Section 2 ΓÇö copy system prompt exactly Implementing intake_agent.py voice parsing Section 3 ΓÇö copy system prompt exactly Implementing morning_briefing.py Section 4 ΓÇö copy system prompt exactly Implementing llm_service.py Section 5.1 ΓÇö copy wrapper code exactly including retry logic Setting up Ollama on Oracle server Section 5.2 ΓÇö follow commands exactly Implementing EmbeddingService Section 5.3 ΓÇö copy with &apos;query:&apos; and &apos;passage:&apos; prefixes Implementing critic_agent.py Section 6 ΓÇö use conflict rules and safety blocks exactly Setting up knowledge ingestion Section 7 ΓÇö enforce all chunk quality rules Implementing offline advisory on Android Section 8 ΓÇö use template system, not LLM for offline 9.2 Prompt Modification Rules ΓÜá Do NOT modify the system prompts in this document without testing. LLM behavior changes unpredictably with wording changes. If you believe a prompt needs modification: (1) Test original prompt first. (2) Document why change is needed. (3) Test modified prompt on 20+ cases. (4) Compare results. Only then update this document. Temperature 0.2 is set for advisory synthesis ΓÇö do not increase. Higher temperature creates inconsistent outputs. Temperature 0.1 for scheme eligibility ΓÇö maximum consistency required for legal-adjacent content. Temperature 0.3 for morning briefings ΓÇö slightly more variety is acceptable here. max_tokens 1500 for advisory ΓÇö sufficient for complete advisory JSON. Do not reduce. Never add &apos;be creative&apos; or &apos;think outside the box&apos; to agricultural prompt instructions. AGRI-SAGE AI AGENT PROMPT HANDBOOK v1.0 Copy prompts exactly. Validate all output. Never trust raw LLM text.

VISION MODEL EVALUATION 4.1 Golden Dataset Structure tests/golden/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ manifest.json # SHA-256 hash of every image ΓÇö frozen Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ README.md # How to add new images to golden set Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ rice/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ blast/ # Minimum 30 images Γöé Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ blast_001.jpg # Filename: {disease}_{number}.jpg Γöé Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ blast_001.json # Label: {label, severity, expert_name, date} Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ brown_spot/ # Minimum 20 images Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ bacterial_blight/ # Minimum 20 images Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ healthy/ # Minimum 30 images Γöé ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ ood/ # Out-of-distribution: minimum 20 images Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ banana/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ fusarium_wilt/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ sigatoka/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ healthy/ Γöé ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ ood/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ coconut/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ wclwd/ # Kerala wilt disease Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ bud_rot/ Γöé ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ healthy/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ tomato/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ early_blight/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ late_blight/ Γöé Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ leaf_curl/ Γöé ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ healthy/ ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ maize/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ northern_leaf_blight/ Γö£ΓöÇΓöÇ common_rust/ ΓööΓöÇΓöÇ healthy/ 4.2 Label JSON Format # tests/golden/rice/blast/blast_001.json { &quot;image_file&quot;: &quot;blast_001.jpg&quot;, &quot;label&quot;: &quot;rice_blast&quot;, &quot;confidence&quot;: &quot;high&quot;, &quot;severity&quot;: &quot;medium&quot;, &quot;affected_area_pct&quot;: 15.0, &quot;growth_stage_visible&quot;: &quot;tillering&quot;, &quot;collection_date&quot;: &quot;2026-08-15&quot;, &quot;collection_location&quot;: &quot;Wayanad, Kerala&quot;, &quot;expert_reviewer&quot;: &quot;Dr. [Name], KAU&quot;, &quot;review_date&quot;: &quot;2026-08-20&quot;, &quot;field_conditions&quot;: &quot;After 3 wet days, 85% humidity&quot;, &quot;image_quality&quot;: &quot;good&quot;, &quot;sha256&quot;: &quot;abc123...&quot; } 4.3 Vision Model Evaluation Script # scripts/evaluate_vision_model.py import onnxruntime as ort import numpy as np from sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report, confusion_matrix from sklearn.calibration import calibration_curve import json, os from pathlib import Path def run_evaluation(model_path: str, golden_dir: str) -&gt; dict: session = ort.InferenceSession(model_path) y_true, y_pred, y_prob = [], [], [] for crop_dir in Path(golden_dir).iterdir(): for disease_dir in crop_dir.iterdir(): if disease_dir.name == &apos;ood&apos;: continue # Evaluate OOD separately for img_file in disease_dir.glob(&apos;*.jpg&apos;): label_file = img_file.with_suffix(&apos;.json&apos;) if not label_file.exists(): continue label = json.loads(label_file.read_text())[&apos;label&apos;] img = preprocess_image(img_file) logits = session.run([&apos;logits&apos;], {&apos;image&apos;: img})[0] probs = softmax(logits[0]) pred_idx = np.argmax(probs) y_true.append(LABEL_TO_IDX[label]) y_pred.append(pred_idx) y_prob.append(probs[pred_idx]) macro_f1 = f1_score(y_true, y_pred, average=&apos;macro&apos;) ece = compute_ece(y_true, y_pred, y_prob) results = { &apos;macro_f1&apos;: macro_f1, &apos;ece&apos;: ece, &apos;classification_report&apos;: classification_report(y_true, y_pred, target_names=ALL_LABELS), &apos;PASS&apos;: macro_f1 &gt;= 0.82 and ece &lt;= 0.12 } if not results[&apos;PASS&apos;]: print(f&apos;FAIL: macro_F1={macro_f1:.3f} (need &gt;=0.82), ECE={ece:.3f} (need &lt;=0.12)&apos;) exit(1) # Fail CI if model does not meet bar return results def compute_ece(y_true, y_pred, y_prob, n_bins=10): # Expected Calibration Error ΓÇö measures confidence reliability from sklearn.calibration import calibration_curve fraction_pos, mean_pred = calibration_curve( [1 if t==p else 0 for t,p in zip(y_true,y_pred)], y_prob, n_bins=n_bins) return np.mean(np.abs(fraction_pos - mean_pred)) 5. LOAD &amp; PERFORMANCE TESTS 5.1 Locust Load Test Configuration # tests/performance/locustfile.py from locust import HttpUser, task, between import json class AgriSAGEFarmerUser(HttpUser): wait_time = between(2, 8) # Realistic farmer usage pattern token = None def on_start(self): # Get auth token response = self.client.post(&apos;/api/v1/auth/verify-otp&apos;, json={&apos;phone&apos;: f&apos;+9199{self.user_id:08d}&apos;, &apos;otp&apos;: &apos;123456&apos;}, name=&apos;auth&apos;) if response.status_code == 200: self.token = response.json()[&apos;access_token&apos;] self.headers = {&apos;Authorization&apos;: f&apos;Bearer {self.token}&apos;} @task(3) def get_home_data(self): self.client.get(&apos;/api/v1/farms&apos;, headers=self.headers, name=&apos;get_farms&apos;) self.client.get(&apos;/api/v1/weather/10.12/76.34&apos;, headers=self.headers, name=&apos;get_weather&apos;) @task(2) def get_risk(self): self.client.get(&apos;/api/v1/risk/test-farm-id&apos;, headers=self.headers, name=&apos;get_risk&apos;) @task(1) def generate_advisory(self): # Most expensive operation ΓÇö lowest frequency self.client.post(&apos;/api/v1/diagnose&apos;, json={&apos;observation_id&apos;: &apos;test-obs-id&apos;}, headers=self.headers, name=&apos;diagnose&apos;) # Run: locust -f locustfile.py --host=https://api.agrisage.in --users=50 --spawn-rate=5 # Target: P95 advisory &lt; 8s | P95 home data &lt; 1s | Error rate &lt; 1% 5.2 Performance Targets Endpoint P50 Target P95 Target P99 Target Error Rate GET /health &lt; 50ms &lt; 100ms &lt; 200ms 0% GET /api/v1/farms &lt; 100ms &lt; 300ms &lt; 500ms &lt; 0.1% GET /api/v1/weather/{lat}/{lon} &lt; 200ms &lt; 500ms &lt; 1000ms &lt; 0.5% GET /api/v1/risk/{farm_id} &lt; 300ms &lt; 800ms &lt; 1500ms &lt; 0.5% POST /api/v1/observations (with image) &lt; 2000ms &lt; 4000ms &lt; 6000ms &lt; 1% POST /api/v1/diagnose &lt; 4000ms &lt; 8000ms &lt; 12000ms &lt; 2% GET /api/v1/schemes &lt; 200ms &lt; 500ms &lt; 1000ms &lt; 0.1% 6.
